# Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim

ברטנורא על משנה כלאים

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Zeraim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Torat-Emet (Segments: 381, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2022 (Segments: 373)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:1)


**הַחִטִּין וְהַזּוּנִין.** קוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי זיוא"ן:


החטין והזונין (wheat and weed growing among wheat, darnel or rye-grass) – we call it ZIYUAN in Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:2)


**אֵינָן כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה.** לְפִי שֶׁכְּשֶׁהִשְׁחִיתוּ דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל אֶת דַּרְכָּם הָיְתָה הָאָרֶץ גַּם הִיא מַזְנָה אֶת פֵּרוֹתֶיהָ וְהָיוּ זוֹרְעִים חִטִּים וּמוֹצִיאָה זוּנִין, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא הֲוֵי כִּלְאַיִם עִם הַחִטִּים. אֲבָל עִם שְׁאָר מִינֵי תְבוּאָה הֲוֵי כִּלְאַיִם, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא חֲזִי לְמַאֲכַל אָדָם, מִכָּל מָקוֹם כֵּיוָן שֶׁמּוֹלִיכִים אוֹתוֹ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם לְמַאֲכַל יוֹנִים הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם:


אינן כלאים זה בזה – because when the generation of the Flood perverted their way, the land became degenerate in his produce for they (i.e., the people) would sow wheat and it would produce weeds, therefore, they were not the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants with the wheat. But with the rest of the kinds of grain, they would be the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants, and even though that it wasn’t appropriate for human consumption, nevertheless, since they bring it from place to place for the consumption of doves, they were the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field (i.e., כלאים).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:3)


**הַשְּׂעֹרִים וְשִׁבֹּלֶת שׁוּעָל.** כָּל הַשְּׁנוּיִם בַּמִּשְׁנָה זוּג זוּג אֵינָם כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה, אֶחָד מִזּוּג זֶה וְאֶחָד מִזּוּג זֶה הֵם כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה. וְהָא דְאָמְרִינַן בִּמְנָחוֹת [דַּף ט.] הַכֻּסְּמִין מִין חִטִּים וְשִׁיפוֹן מִין שְׂעוֹרִים, הַנֵּי מִילֵי לְעִנְיַן חַלָּה שֶׁמִּצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה לְשִׁעוּר חַלָּה, אֲבָל לְעִנְיַן כִּלְאַיִם הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה:


השעורים ושבולת שועל – all that is taught in this Mishnah, pair by pair are not the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field with each other, [but] one from this pair and one from that pair are the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field with each other. And that which we stated in [Tractate] Menahot [9a] that the spelt is a species of wheat and rye is a species of barley, these words, as regards Hallah that combine one with the other for the measurement of Hallah, but concerning the issue of the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants, they would be the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants with each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:4)


**וְשִׁבֹּלֶת שׁוּעָל.** שְׂעוֹרִים מִדְבָּרִיִּים וּבְלַעַ"ז אוינ"א:


ושבולת שועל – desert barley and in the foreign tongue, OYNAH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:5)


**כֻּסְמִין.** בִּלְשׁוֹן לַעַ"ז אספילט"א:


כוסמין (spelt) – In the foreign tongue ASPILTAH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:6)


**שִׁיפוֹן.** בְּלַעַ"ז סיגל"א. וּמִשּׁוּם דְּכֻסְמִין וְשִׁיפוֹן דָּמוּ לַהֲדָדֵי אֵינָם כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה:


שיפון (rye; some say oats) – in the foreign tongue, SIGLAH, but because spelt and rye [or oats] are similar to each other they are not the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field with each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:7)


**וְהַסַּפִּיר.** יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים ציצרקל"א בְּלַעַ"ז. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי מא"ש:


והספיר (name of a species of beans) – there are those who say that it is TZITZERKALA in the foreign tongue. But Maimonides explained that we call it MA’AS in Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:8)


**הַפֻּרְקְדָן.** רַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא זֶרַע דַּק שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי גילבא"ן:


הפורקדן (species of peas) – Maimonides explained that it is thin seed that we call GILBAN In Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:9)


**וְטֹפֵחַ.** הוּא קִטְנִית שֶׁגַּרְגְּרָיו עֲגֻלִּים לְבָנִים וְקוֹרִין לוֹ קורטמא"ן. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁטֹּפֵחַ הוּא שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ גילבא"ן:


וטופח (bean) – it is a small legume whose berries are round and we call it KORTEMAN, but I heard that טופח is what they call GILBAN (i.e., a species of bean/pea).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:10)


**וְהַשְּׁעוּעִית.** קוֹרִין לָהּ בַּעֲרָבִי לוביא"ה:


והשעועית (species of bean, kidney-bean) we call it LOBIAH in Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:1:11)


**אֵינָם כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה.** אִם זָרַע כָּל אֶחָד עִם בֶּן זוּגוֹ:


אינם כלאים זה בזה- if he sowed each of them with its partner.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:1)


**הַקִּשּׁוּת.** קִשּׁוּאִין שֶׁקּוֹרִין בַּעֲרָבִי פאקו"ס וּבְלַעַ"ז קוקומברו"ש:


הקישות (chatmelon)– cucumbers that they call them PAKKUS in Arabic and KOKKOMBROSH in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:2)


**וְהַמְּלָפְפוֹנוֹת.** נִקְרָאִים בַּעֲרָבִי כייא"ר וּבְלַעַ"ז ציטרול"י:


והמלפפונות (yellow mellon) – they are called KIYYAR in Arabic and TZITROLI in the foreign tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כִּלְאָיִם.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:4)


**חֲזֶרֶת וַחֲזֶרֶת גַּלִּים.** הַחַסָּא הַגְּדֵלָה בַּגַּנִּים וְחַסָּא הַגְּדֵלָה בֶּהָרִים:


חזרת וחזרת גלים - the lettuce that grows in the garden and the lettuce that grows in the mountains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:5)


**עֻלְשִׁים.** שֶׁל גַּנִּים וְעֻלְשֵׁי שָׂדֶה, וְנִקְרָאִים בַּעֲרָבִי הנדב"י:


עולשים (endives) – of gardens and wild/field endives and they are called HENDBEE In Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:6)


**כְּרֵשִׁין.** שֶׁל גִּנָּה וּכְרֵשֵׁי שָׂדֶה, וְהֵם הַנִּקְרָאִים בַּעֲרָבִי כורא"ת וּבְלַעַ"ז פורו"ש:


כרישין (leeks) – of gardens and leeks of the field and hey are called KORAT in Arabic and POROISH in the foreign tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:7)


**כֻּסְבָּר.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי כוליינדר"ו בְּלַעַ"ז. תַּרְגּוּם יְרוּשַׁלְמִי כְּזֶרַע גַּד, כְּבַר זְרַע כֻּסְבַּר:


כוסבר (coriander) – that is its name in Arabic, KULIANDRO in the foreign tongue. The Jerusalem Targum (quoting Exodus 16:31): “[The house of Israel named it manna;] it was like coriander seed, [white and it tasted like wafers in honey],” like the prairie (i.e., wild) seed of coriander.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:8)


**וְהָרְמוּצָה.** דְּלַעַת מָרָה שֶׁמְּמַתְּקִים אוֹתָהּ בְּרֶמֶץ כְּלוֹמַר בְּאֵפֶר חַם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ רִשְׁפֵי אֵשׁ לְפִיכָךְ קוֹרִין לָהּ רְמוּצָה:


והרמוצה – a bitter gourd (i.e., a general name for cucumbers and pumpkins) that we sweeten it in hot ashes, that is to say, in hot ashes that have in it flames/sparks of fire, therefore we call it רמוצה .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:9)


**וּפוֹל הַמִּצְרִי.** פאסול"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


ופול המצרין – PASULI in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:2:10)


**וְהֶחָרוּב.** מִין קִטְנִית, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ חָרוּב לְפִי שֶׁתַּרְמִילָיו שֶׁהַזֶּרַע בְּתוֹכוֹ הֵן דַּקִּין וַעֲקֻמִּין כְּחָרוּבִין, וְהוּא מִמִּין פּוֹל הַמִּצְרִי:


והחרוב (carob) – a kind of legume, and we call it a carob because of the pod that the seed within are thin and curved like the carobs and it is of the species of the Egyptian bean.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:1)


**וְהַנָּפוּץ.** הוּא מִין צְנוֹן וְעָלָיו דּוֹמִים לַעֲלֵי הַלֶּפֶת, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם:


והנפוץ (a kind of radish, resembling the carrot as to foliage, and the radish as to its taste) – it is species of radish and its leaves are similar to the leaves of a turnip, therefore it does not have the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field with each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:2)


**וְהַתְּרוֹבְתוֹר.** מִין כְּרוּב שֶׁקְּלָחִים שֶׁלּוֹ דַּקִּים:


והתרובתור – a species of cabbage whose stalks are thin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:3)


**תְּרָדִין.** בַּעֲרָבִי סִילְקָ"א וּבְלַעַ"ז בליט"י:


תרדין (a species of beet) – in Arabic SILKA, and in the foreign language BLITI.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:4)


**לְעוּנִין.** בַּעֲרָבִי קט"ף וּבְלַעַ"ז ארמולא"ש:


לעונין (garden orache) – in Arabic KATAF and in the foreign tongue ARMULASH..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:5)


**הַשּׁוּמָנִית.** שׁוּם מִדְבָּרִי וְהוּא קָטָן מִן הַשּׁוּם הַגָּדֵל בַּגַּנִּים:


השומנית – desert garlic and it is smaller than the garlic that grows in gardens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:6)


**בְּצַלְצוּל.** בָּצָל מִדְבָּרִי קָטָן מִשְּׁאָר בְּצָלִים לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרָא בְּצַלְצוּל:


בצלצול – a desert onion that is smaller than the rest of the onions, therefore it is called בצלצול.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:3:7)


**פְּלַסְלוֹס.** מִין תֻּרְמוֹס הוּא. וְתֻרְמוֹס מִין קִטְנִית יָדוּעַ מַר מְאֹד שֶׁשּׁוֹלְקִים אוֹתוֹ שֶׁבַע פְּעָמִים וְאוֹכְלִים אוֹתוֹ בְּקִנּוּחַ סְעֻדָּה:


פלסלוס (a sort of lupine) – it is a species of lupine/תורמוס and the lupine is a species of known pulse/beans that is very bitter that they seethe/boil it seven times and eat it as a dessert.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:1)


**אֲגָסִים.** בַּעֲרָבִי אגא"ס וּבְלַעַ"ז פירא"ש:


אגסים – in Arabic GAS and in the foreign language PREASH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:2)


**קְרוּסְטוּמֵלִין.** מִין אֲגָסִים קְטַנִּים הַדּוֹמִים לַעֲפָצִים שֶׁקּוֹרִין מִילִין:


קרוסטומלין (Crustumenian pear – red on one side) – it is of the species of small pears that are similar to gall-nuts that we call a species of oak from which the gall-nut is collected.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:3)


**פָּרִישִׁים.** הֵם חֲבוּשִׁים, בַּעֲרָבִי ספרג"ל וּבְלַעַ"ז קודוניי"ש:


פרישים - they are quince, SPERGEL in Arabic and KODONISH in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:4)


**עֻזְרָדִים.** בַּעֲרָבִי זערו"ד וּבְלַעַ"ז סורבא"ש:


עוזרדים (crab-apple/sorb-apple) – in Arabic ZAAROD and in the foreign language SORBASH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:5)


**חַזְרָד.** תַּפּוּחַ יְעָרִי, תַּרְגּוּם וְכַפְתּוֹר וְחִיזָר:


חזרד (crab-apple; similar yet heterogeneous to apple) – a forest apple, it is the Aramaic of an ornament of the candlestick in the shape of a pomegranate that is prickly/thorny.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:6)


**אֲפַרְסְקִים.** בְּלַעַ"ז פירשאג"ס, וּכְשֶׁהֵן קְטַנִּים דּוֹמִין לִשְׁקֵדִים:


אפרסקים (peaches) – in the foreign language PIRASHAGAS, which are small and similar to almonds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:7)


**שִׁזָּפִין.** הִרְכִּיב זֵיתִים עַל רִמּוֹן נָפֵיק מִנְּהוֹן שִׁזָּפִין:


שזפין (jujubes) – he engrafts olives on the pomegranates, and jujubes come out from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:4:8)


**רִימִין.** בֶּעָרוּךְ פֵּרֵשׁ פולצדקא"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


רימין (lote/wild jujubes) – In the Arukh, it explains PULTADKAI – in the foreign language.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:5:1)


**הַצְּנוֹן וְהַנָּפוּץ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַפְּרִי וְהֶעָלִים דּוֹמִין זֶה לָזֶה הֵן כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה לְפִי שֶׁאֵין טַעַם הַפְּרִי דּוֹמֶה, שֶׁהָלְכוּ בָּזֶה אַחַר טַעַם הַפְּרִי:


הצנון והנפוץ (a radish and a kind of radish) – even though the fruit and he leaves ae similar one to another, they are a forbidden junction of heterogenous plants to each other [in the same field] because the taste of the fruit is not similar for in this they went after the taste of the fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:5:2)


**לַפְסָן.** פֵּרֵשׁ בֶּעָרוּךְ מרוי"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


לפסן (charlock, a plant resembling the mustard plant) – in the Arukh it is expressed MARVIV in the foreign language.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:1)


**כֶּלֶב כֻּפְרִי.** כֶּלֶב שֶׁמְּגַדְּלִים בְּנֵי הַכְּפָרִים וְהוּא קָטָן וְדוֹמֶה לְשׁוּעָל:


כלב כופרי – a dog that the people of villages raise and he is small and similar to a fox.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:2)


**הַיְּעֵלִים.** מִין חַיָּה, וְאַקּוֹ מְתַרְגְּמִין וְיַעֲלָא:


היעלים (mountain-goat, wild goat) – a species of wild life and ibex/ is translated in Aramaic as a wild/mountain goat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:6:3)


**עָרוֹד.** חֲמוֹר הַבַּר:


ערוד – the prairie donkey.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:1)


**אֵין מְבִיאִין אִילָן בְּאִילָן.** אֵין מַרְכִּיבִין אִילַן מַאֲכָל בְּאִילַן מַאֲכָל מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ, אוֹ אִילַן סְרַק בְּאִילַן מַאֲכָל, אֲבָל אִילַן סְרַק בְּאִילַן סְרַק כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין שׁוּם אֶחָד מֵהֶם עוֹשֶׂה פְּרִי, כְּמִין אֶחָד חָשׁוּב וְשָׁרֵי:


אין מביאין אילן באילן – we don’t engraft a tree with edible fruit with another tree of edible fruit of one type mixed with another, or a tree that bears no fruit with a tree with edible fruit, but a tree that bears no fruit with another tree that bears no fruit, because not a single one of them makes [edible] fruit is considered like one species and it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:2)


**יָרָק בְּיָרָק.** מִין שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:7:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר יָרָק בְּאִילָן.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה מתיר ירק באילן – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:1)


**שִׁקְמָה.** אִילַן תְּאֵנָה שֶׁגָּדֵל בַּיְעָרִים:


שקמה – a fig tree that grows in forests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:2)


**סַדָּן שֶׁל שִׁקְמָה.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנֶּחְתַּךְ וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ שָׁרָשָׁיו בָּאָרֶץ נִקְרָא סַדָּן, וְאֵין נוֹטְעִין יָרָק לְתוֹכוֹ דַּהֲוֵי יָרָק בְּאִילָן:


סדן של שקמה – after it was cut off and there remained in the ground its roots it is called סדן/trunk . But we don’t plant a vegetable into it for it would be [engrafting] a vegetable onto a tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:3)


**פֵּיגָם.** עֵשֶׂב שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ רוד"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


פיגם (the herb rue) – a grass that we call it RODA in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:4)


**קִדָּה.** מִין אִילָן שֶׁל בְּשָׂמִים תַּרְגּוּם קִדָּה קְצִיעָה, וּכְתִיב (תהלים מה) מֹר וַאֲהָלוֹת קְצִיעוֹת כָּל בִּגְדֹתֶיךָ:


קידה (bark of a tree called cassia) – it is species of tree of spices; the Aramaic translation of קדה is קציעה/an aromatic bark, an ingredient of frankincense, and it is written (Psalms 45:9): “All your robes [are fragrant] with myrrh and aloes and cassia.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:5)


**חֲצוּב.** מִין יָרָק שֶׁשָּׁרָשָׁיו יוֹרְדִים בְּעֹמֶק הָאָרֶץ בְּיֹשֶׁר וְאֵינָם נוֹטִים לְכָאן וּלְכָאן, וּבוֹ תִּחֵם יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אֶת הָאָרֶץ:


חצוב (a shrubby plant with deep and straight roots, used for hedges to mark boundaries/cistus) – a species of vegetable whose roots go straight down into the depth of the earth and they do not turn to this way or that, and through it Joshua marked the limits of the Land [of Canaan] (see Talmud Bava Batra 56a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:6)


**מְקֵרוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן קֹר שֶׁתְּהֵא הַתְּאֵנָה מְקָרֶרֶת הַחֲצוּב שֶׁהוּא חַם בְּיוֹתֵר. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחַר שֶׁתִּהְיֶה עָלָיו לְצֵל לְשׁוֹן קוֹרָה:


מקירו – the language of cold/cooling that the fig cools off the cistus which is hotter. Another explanation: that its leaves will be shade, the language of cooling/קורה .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:7)


**מֵימֶיהָ לְתוֹכָהּ.** שֶׁהָאֲבַטִּיחַ לַח וּמָלֵא מַיִם:


מימיו לתוכה – for the watermelon is moist and filled with water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:8:8)


**חַלָּמִית.** בַּעֲרָבִי כובוז"א וּבְלַעַ"ז מלוו"א:


חלמית (mucilaginous juice of mallows – used for he preservation of gourd seed) – in Arabic KUBUZA and in the foreign tongue MALUVA.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:1)


**הַטּוֹמֵן לֶפֶת וּצְנוֹנוֹת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נִשְׁמָרִים תַּחַת הַקַּרְקַע, שֶׁלֹּא כְדֶרֶךְ שְׁתִילָה, גַּלֵּי דַעְתֵּיהּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה בְּהַשְׁרָשָׁתָן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁטָּמַן אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁל לֶפֶת וַאֲגֻדָּה שֶׁל צְנוֹנוֹת יַחַד:


הטומן לפת וצנונות (who hides/buries turnips or radishes) – in order that they be protected under the ground which is not the manner of planting, he revealed his intention that he has no interest in their taking root, as for example, that he buries a bunch of turnips or a bunch of radishes together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:2)


**אִם הָיוּ מִקְצָת עָלִין מְגֻלִּין.** מִשּׁוּם שַׁבָּת נָקְטֵיהּ, דְּאִם הָיוּ כָּל הֶעָלִין מְכֻסִּין אִי אֶפְשָׁר לִטֹּל אִם לֹא שֶׁיָּזִיז עָפָר בְּיָדַיִם:


אם היו מקצת עליו מגולין – it (i.e., the Mishnah) took this because of [the laws of] Shabbat, but if all of the leaves were covered, it would be impossible to take it if he didn’t move the dirt with his hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:3)


**לֹא מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם שְׁבִיעִית.** לֹא מִשּׁוּם זוֹרֵעַ כִּלְאַיִם וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם זוֹרֵעַ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית. אִי נַמִּי אֲפִלּוּ הוֹסִיף מָאתַיִם מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם שָׁרֵי דְּלָאו דֶּרֶךְ זְרִיעָה [הוּא], וְאִם הָיָה מִשִּׁשִּׁית וְנִכְנְסוּ לַשְּׁבִיעִית וְהוֹצִיאוּ סְפִיחִים כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמּוֹסִיפִים הַבְּצָלִים בַּתָּלוּשׁ, אֵין בָּהֶם מִשּׁוּם סְפִיחֵי שְׁבִיעִית:


לא משום כלאים ולא משום שביעית – not because of sowing the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field and not because of sowing during the Sabbatical year [of rest]. Alternatively, even if he added two hundred because of the forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field, it is permitted, for [this] is not the manner of sowing, but if it was in the from the sixth [year] and entering into the Seventh Year, and they removed the after-growths in the manner that they add the onions into that which is detached from the ground, they don’t have anything because of the Seventh-year after-growths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:4)


**וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם מַעַשְׂרוֹת.** אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְעַשֵּׂר כְּשֶׁיִּתְלוֹשׁ אוֹתָם מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהִטְמִינָם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְכָל מַעְשַׂר הָאָרֶץ מִזֶּרַע הָאָרֶץ, וְהַאי לָאו זְרִיעָה הִיא:


ולא משום מעשרות – he is not required to tithe when he detached them from the place that he hid them, as it is written (Leviticus 27:30): “All tithes from the land, whether seed from the ground [or fruit from the tree, are the LORD’s], but this is not sowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:5)


**וְנִטָּלִים בְּשַׁבָּת.** דְּכִתְלוּשִׁים דָּמוּ, וְאוֹחֵז בֶּעָלִים הַמְגֻלִּים וּמוֹצִיאָן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמֵזִיז עָפָר מִמְּקוֹמוֹ, טִלְטוּל מִן הַצַּד לְצֹרֶךְ דְּבַר הֶתֵּר וְאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בַּדָּבָר הָאָסוּר מֻתָּר:


וניטלים בשבת – for they are [considered] like that which is detached [from the ground,” and he holds on to the revealed leaves and removes them. But even though that he moves the dirt from its place, it is carrying from the side for the needs of something permitted and it doesn’t touch that which is prohibited is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 1:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/1:9:6)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁנֵי חִטִּים וּשְׂעוֹרָה.** טַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יט) שָׂדְךָ לֹא תִזְרַע כִּלְאָיִם, וְסָבַר דְּקַרְקַע בְּלֹא זֶרַע לֹא אִקְּרֵי שָׂדֶה, וּמַשְׁמַע לֹא תִזְרַע כִּלְאַיִם בַּהֲדֵי שָׂדְךָ שֶׁנִּזְרַע כְּבָר. וְכֵן (דברים כב) לֹא תִזְרַע כַּרְמְךָ כִּלְאָיִם, לֹא תִזְרַע כִּלְאַיִם דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁנֵי מִינֵי זְרָעִים בַּהֲדֵי כַּרְמְךָ, וְלֹא מְחַיֵּב בְּכִלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם עַד שֶׁיִּזְרַע חִטָּה וּשְׂעוֹרָה וְחַרְצָן בְּמַפֹּלֶת יָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. דְּקַרְקַע אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ זְרוּעָה קָרוּי שָׂדֶה, אֲבָל אֵינוֹ קָרוּי כֶּרֶם בְּלֹא חַרְצָן, הִלְכָּךְ הַזּוֹרֵעַ חִטָּה וּשְׂעוֹרָה כְּאֶחָד חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאֵי זְרָעִים, וְאֵינוֹ חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם עַד שֶׁיִּזְרַע חִטָּה וּשְׂעוֹרָה וְחַרְצָן בְּמַפֹּלֶת יָד:


עד שיהיו שני חטים ושעורה – The reason of Rabbi Yehuda is because as it is written (Leviticus 19:19): “you shall not sow your field with two kinds of seed,” and he holds that land without seed is not called a field, and that implies that you shall not sow with two kinds of seeds while your field is already sown, and similarly, (Deuteronomy 22:9): “you shall not sow your vineyard with a second kind of seed,” you shall not sow two kinds of seeds – which are two kinds of seeds together with your vineyard and you are not liable for food crops in a vineyard until he sows wheat and barley and/or shells of grapes strewing the seed with one’s hand. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda. For land, even though it is not sown is called a field, but it is not called a vineyard without shells of grapes, therefore, a person who sows wheat and barley together is liable for the prohibited mixture of seeds, but is not liable for [prohibited] food crops in a vineyard until he sows wheat and barley and the pomace of grapes strewing the seed with one’s hand.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:1)


**כָּל סְאָה שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהּ רֹבַע.** הַקַּב דְּהַיְנוּ אֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע לִסְאָה, שֶׁהַסְּאָה שִׁשָּׁה קַבִּין:


כל סאה שיש בה רבע – The Kab (=1/6 of a Seah or 24 egg bulks) which is one in twenty-four parts of a Seah, and the Seah is six Kabim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:2)


**יְמַעֵט.** אוֹתוֹ מִין אַחֵר הַמְעֹרָב בּוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּפְחַת הָרֹבַע וְיִתְבַּטֵּל בְּתוֹךְ הַסְּאָה, וּמֻתָּר לִזְרֹעַ וְלֹא מִתְּסַר מִשּׁוּם זוֹרֵעַ כִּלְאַיִם:


ימעט - [he shall diminish/reduce] that other species that is mixed in it until the quarter [of a Kab] would be diminished and will be nullified within the Seah and he would be permitted to sow and not be prohibited because of sowing a forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants in the same field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:3)


**יָבוֹר.** מִכֵּיוָן שֶׁהֻזְקַק לְמַעֵט יָסִיר הַכֹּל, מִשּׁוּם דְּמֵחֲזֵי כִּמְקַיֵּם כִּלְאַיִם מִכֵּיוָן שֶׁהִתְחִיל לִבְרֹר אִם לֹא הָיָה בּוֹרֵר אֶת כֻּלּוֹ. אֲבָל תְּחִלָּתוֹ פָּחוֹת מֵרֹבַע שָׁרֵי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


יבור – for since it was necessary to diminish, he removes everything, because I appears like he is establishing a forbidden junction of heterogeneous plants/diverse kinds in the same field, for since he began to sift, if he would not sift everything. But if at its outside it is less than one-quarter [of a Kab] it is permitted. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:4)


**בֵּין מִמִּין אֶחָד.** בֵּין שֶׁאוֹתוֹ רֹבַע מִמִּין אֶחָד בֵּין מִמִּינִין הַרְבֵּה צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט:


בין ממין אחד – whether in that ssame quarter from one species or whether from many species, he must diminish/reduce it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:5)


**לֹא אָמְרוּ אֶלָּא מִמִּין אֶחָד.** דְּאֵין שְׁנֵי מִינִין מִצְטָרְפִין לְשִׁעוּר רֹבַע:


לא אמרו אלא ממן אחד – for two species do not combine to the measurement of a quarter [of a Kab].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:1:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כָּל שֶׁהוּא כִלְאַיִם לַסְּאָה.** אִיכָּא בֵּין חֲכָמִים לְתַנָּא קַמָּא כְּגוֹן סְאָה שְׂעוֹרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ רֹבַע שִׁבֹּלֶת שׁוּעָל וְכֻסְּמִין, לְתַנָּא קַמָּא צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט, לַחֲכָמִים אֵין צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט, דְּאֵין שִׁבֹּלֶת שׁוּעָל מִצְטָרֵף לְכֻסְּמִין כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם בִּסְאָה שֶׁל שְׂעוֹרִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים כל שהוא כלאים לסאה – there is [a difference] between the Sages and the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] as for example, a Seah of barley that has in it a quarter [of a Kab] of oats and spelt, [according] to the first Tanna/teacher, one needs to diminish it; to the Sages, oe does not have to diminish it, for oats do not combine with spelt since they are not diverse kinds in the Seah of barley. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:1)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים תְּבוּאָה בִתְבוּאָה.** תְּבוּאָה בְּמִין תְּבוּאָה אוֹ בְקִטְנִית הוּא דְאָמְרָן בְּרֹבַע לִסְאָה יְמַעֵט, אֲבָל זֵרְעוֹנֵי גִנָּה שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בִתְבוּאָה אוֹ בְקִטְנִית:


במה דברים אמורים תבואה בתבואה – grain [is mixed] with a species of grain or pulse that we said in one-fourth of a Kab per Seah, he should diminish/reduce it, but garden seeds [which are not used for food (i.e., seeds of vegetables) that were combined/mixed with grain or pulse [combine to form an amount sufficient to prohibit the sowing of the Seah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:2)


**אֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בְּנוֹפֵל.** מֵהֶן לְבֵית סְאָה חִטִּין, כְּלוֹמַר כְּשִׁעוּר הַזֶּרַע שֶׁזּוֹרְעִים מֵהֶן לְמָקוֹם שֶׁזּוֹרְעִין בּוֹ סְאָה שֶׁל חִטִּין. וְשִׁעוּר הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁזּוֹרְעִים בּוֹ סְאָה שֶׁל חִטִּין הוּא חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה עַל חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה, וְאֵין זוֹרְעִים בּוֹ מִזֵּרְעוֹנֵי גִנָּה אֶלָּא קַב וָחֵצִי לְפִי שֶׁהֵן דַּקִּין וּבְקַב וָחֵצִי מֵהֶן יֵשׁ בָּהֶן כְּדֵי לִזְרֹעַ מָקוֹם שֶׁזּוֹרְעִים בּוֹ סְאָה שֶׁל חִטִּין. נִמְצָא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה שֶׁאִם נִתְעָרֵב בִּסְאָה אַחַת שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה אוֹ שֶׁל קִטְנִית אוֹ שֶׁל זֵרְעוֹנִים אֶחָד מֵאַרְבָּעָה וְעֶשְׂרִים שֶׁל קַב וָחֵצִי מִזֵּרְעוֹנֵי גִנָּה שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מִשִּׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּקַב חַיָּב לְמַעֵט. הִלְכָּךְ אִם נִתְעָרֵב בִּסְאָה תְבוּאָה כַּבֵּיצָה וָחֵצִי זֵרְעוֹנֵי גִנָּה שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בְּקַב וַחֲצִי קַב, יְמַעֵט:


מעשרים וארבע בנופל – from them to the Bet Seah (i.e., 50 cubits by 50 cubits/2500 square cubits) of wheat, that is to say, according to the measure of seed that they sow from them for the place that they sow in it a Seah of wheat. And the measure of the place that they sow a Seah of wheat is fifty cubits by fifty cubits but they don’t sow in it from the seeds of a garden other than a Kab and one-half because they are thin, and a Kab and one-half from them there is sufficient to sow a place that they sow a Seah of wheat. It is found that according to this calculation, that if it was mixed in one Seah of grain or pulse or rows of plants in one bed (i.e., seeds) which are one-in twenty-four of a Kab and an a half from garden seeds which is one of sixteen in a Kab, it is necessary to diminish/reduce them. Therefore, if there was combined in a Seah of grain the volume of an egg and half of garden seeds, which is one out of twenty-four of a Kab and one-half of a Kab, he must reduce it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:2:3)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאָמְרוּ לְהַחְמִיר.** דְּזֵרְעוֹנִים דַּקִּים אוֹסְרִים בְּשִׁעוּר מֻעָט בְּאֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בְּנוֹפֵל מֵהֶן לְבֵית סְאָה, כָּךְ אָמְרוּ לְהָקֵל דְּזֵרְעוֹנִים גַּסִּים שֶׁצָּרִיךְ מֵהֶן הַרְבֵּה לִזְרֹעַ בְּבֵית סְאָה אֵינָן אוֹסְרִין בִּסְאָה חִטִּין אֶלָּא אֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בְּנוֹפֵל מֵהֶן לְבֵית סְאָה, כְּגוֹן זֶרַע פִּשְׁתָּן שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִים לְבֵית סְאָה חִטִּים אֵין צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט עַד שֶׁיִּתְעָרֵב מֵהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ רְבִיעִים בִּסְאָה, וְהַיְנוּ דְקָתָנֵי הַפִּשְׁתָּן בַּתְּבוּאָה מִצְטָרֶפֶת אֶחָד מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בְּנוֹפֵל מֵהֶן לְבֵית סְאָה, וְכֵיוָן דְּנוֹפֵל מֵהֶן שְׁלֹשָׁה סְאִין לְבֵית סְאָה, דְּהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, נִמְצָא שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט עַד שֶׁיִּתְעָרֵב מֵהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ רְבִיעִים שֶׁל קַב בִּסְאָה, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


כשם שאמרו להחמיר – that thin seeds prohibit in a small measurement with one in twenty-four which fall from them into a Bet Seah (i.e., 2500 square cubits), such they said to be lenient for thick seeds that require many of them to sow in a Bet Seah do not forbid in a Seah of wheat other than one in twenty-four when it falls from them into a Beth Seah, such as seed of flax that requires three Seah for a Bet Seah of wheat, there is no need to diminish it until it combines with them three-fourth [a Kab] in a Seah, which means that it is taught that the flax in the grain combines one in twenty-four when it falls into a Bet Seah, and since three Seah falls from them into a Bet Seah, and we state in the Jerusalem Talmud that it is found that there is no need to reduce it until there would combine from them three-foruths of a Kab into the Seah, and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:1)


**עַד שֶׁתַּתְלִיעַ.** וְשִׁעוּרָהּ הֲוֵי כְּשֶׁתִּשְׁהֶה בָּאָרֶץ שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים בְּקַרְקַע לַחָה, וּבִיבֵשָׁה צָרִיךְ יוֹתֵר:


עד שתתליע (until the seed in the ground begins to rot) – and its measure is when it stands still in the ground three days in moist soil but in dry [soil] it requires more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:2)


**וְיוֹפַךְ.** יַהֲפֹךְ הַקַּרְקַע בַּמַּחֲרֵשָׁה בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁלֹּא יִצְמָח הַזֶּרַע:


ויופך – he will turn over he soil with a plow so that the seed will not sprout/grow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:3)


**כַּמָּה יְהֵא חוֹרֵשׁ.** שֶׁלֹּא תֹאמַר שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ לַחֲרֹשׁ תְּלָמִים תְּלָמִים קְטַנִּים סְמוּכִים זֶה לָזֶה, אֶלָּא כְּתַלְמֵי הָרְבִיעָה, כִּתְלָמִים שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לַחֲרֹשׁ אַחַר שֶׁתֵּרֵד הָרְבִיעָה, שֶׁחוֹרְשִׁים תְּלָמִים גְּדוֹלִים:


כמה יהא חורש – so that you will not say that you to plow small ridges/furrows that are adjacent to each other, but rather like the furrows of [fructification from] rainfall like the furrows that people plow after the rainfall occurs when they plow the big furrows.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:3:4)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְשַׁיֵּר רֹבַע לְבֵית סְאָה.** שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר בָּאָרֶץ מָקוֹם שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְהֻפָּךְ אֶלָּא פָּחוֹת מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁהוּא חוֹרֵשׁ, דְּהַיְנוּ רֹבַע קַב לְבֵית סְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל:


כדי שלא ישייר רובע לבית סאה – that there will not remain in the land a place which is not turned over other than less than one-twenty-fourth in the ground when he plows which is a quarter of a Kab for a Bet Seah (i.e., 2500 square cubits – the minimum area required in order to sow one Seah of produce)



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:4:1)


**זְרוּעָה וְנִמְלַךְ לְנָטְעָהּ.** שֶׁהָיְתָה זְרוּעָה תְבוּאָה וְנִמְלַךְ לְנָטְעָהּ גְּפָנִים:


זרועה ונמלך לנוטעה – that it was sown with grain [or vegetables] and he changed his mind to plant it with vines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:4:2)


**גּוֹמֵם.** לְשׁוֹן גּוֹמְמוֹ עִם הַשּׁוֹפִי דְּפֶרֶק גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה [חֻלִּין צב:], כְּלוֹמַר כּוֹרֵת הַגֶּפֶן סָמוּךְ לַקַּרְקַע שֶׁלֹּא יְשַׁיֵּר גָּבוֹהַּ מִן הַקַּרְקַע טֶפַח:


גומם – the language of “peeling the fat off even with the cap” of the chapter [in Tractate Hullin 92b] “The Sciatic Nerve”/גיד הנשה, that is to say, to cut off the vine close to the ground so that it will not remain a handbreadth above the ground.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:1)


**קַנְבּוֹס אוֹ לוּף.** שֶׁמִּינִים הַלָּלוּ אֵין הַתְלָעָה וַחֲרִישָׁה מוֹעֶלֶת בָּהֶן, לְפִי שֶׁמִּתְקַיְּמִים בָּאָרֶץ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים וְאֵינָן מַתְלִיעִין:


קנבוס או לוף (hemp or lof/a plant similar to Colocasia, with edible beans and root, and bearing beans – similar to an onion) – these species, the rottenness/being worn-eaten and the plowing do not affect them, because they are preserved in the ground for three years and do not become worm-eaten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:2)


**אִסְטִיס.** צִבְעוֹ דּוֹמֶה לִתְכֵלֶת, קוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי ני"ל וּבְלַעַ"ז אנדיק"ו, וּרְגִילִים שֶׁכּוֹרְתִים אוֹתוֹ וְחוֹזֵר וְצוֹמֵחַ, וּמַה שֶּׁצּוֹמֵחַ פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה נִקְרָא סָפִיחַ. אִי נַמִּי מַה שֶּׁצּוֹמֵחַ מִן הַזֶּרַע הַנּוֹפֵל בִּשְׁעַת קְצִירָה קָרוּי סָפִיחַ:


אסטיס (woad, a plant producing a deep blue dye) – its color is similar to the purple-blue thread used for ritual fringes (i.e., bluish or cerulean dye) that we call it NIL in Arabic and in the foreign tongue ENDIKO, and we regularly sever it and it returns and grows back, and what grows the second time is called an after-growth/spontaneous growth. Alternatively, that which grows from the seed that falls at the time of harvesting/reaping is called after-growth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:3)


**מְקוֹם הַגְּרָנוֹת.** שֶׁדָּשִׁים בּוֹ תְּבוּאָה וְקִטְנִית:


מקום הגרנות – that they thresh grain and pulse in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:4)


**תִּלְתָּן.** בַּעֲרָבִי חולב"א וּבְלַעַ"ז פינגריק"ו:


תלתן – in Arabic CHULBAH and in the foreign tongue FENGREEKO (i.e., fenugreek)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:5)


**אֵין מְחַיְּבִין אוֹתוֹ לְנַכֵּשׁ.** דִּבְלָאו הָכִי סוֹפוֹ לְעָקְרָם, לְפִי שֶׁאִסְטִיס קָשֶׁה לַתְּבוּאָה וְהָעֲשָׂבִים קָשִׁים לַתִּלְתָּן כְּשֶׁנִּזְרַע לְמַאֲכַל אָדָם, וּמְקוֹם הַגְּרָנוֹת נַמִּי קָשִׁים לוֹ הַזְּרָעִים שֶׁמַּחְלִידִים אֶת הַקַּרְקַע וּמַלְקִים אוֹתָהּ וְלֹא חֲזִי תּוּ לִמְקוֹם דִּישָׁה:


אין מחייבין אותו לנכש (the law does not bind him to pluck out/weed the plants that grow among the fenugreek) – that without that its end would be to uproot them because woad is difficult for the grain for the grasses are hard for fenugreek when it is planted for human food, and the place of the threshing floors – the seeds are hard for it for they undermine the ground and blight/smite it and it is not appropriate further as a place of threshing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:6)


**וְאִם נִכֵּשׁ אוֹ כִסַּח.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא גַּלֵּי דַעְתֵּיהּ דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּאוֹתָם הַנִּשְׁאָרִים שֶׁהֲרֵי עוֹקֵר הַכֹּל חוּץ מִזֶּה, אוֹמְרִים לוֹ עֲקֹר אֶת הַכֹּל, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא נִרְאֶה כִּמְקַיֵּם כִּלְאַיִם:


ואם נכש או כסח (if he weeded or cut down) – for now he has revealed his opinion that it is pleasant for him with those that remain for behold he has uprooted everything except for them, we say to him, uproot everything because it appears like he is preserving mixed seeds/Kilayim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:7)


**נִכֵּשׁ.** שֶׁעָקַר הַצְּמָחִים בְּיָדָיו עִם הַשֹּׁרֶשׁ:


נכש – that he uprooted the plants with the root by his hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:5:8)


**כִּסַּח.** שֶׁחָתַךְ הֶעָלִים וְהַשֹּׁרֶשׁ נִשְׁאַר בָּאָרֶץ, תַּרְגּוּם לֹא תִזְמֹר לֹא תְכַסַּח:


כסח – that he severed the leaves, but the root remains in the ground, the Aramaic translation of (Leviticus 25:4): "לא תזמר" /or prune [your vineyard] is לא תכסח /do not trim/cut.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:1)


**מֵשָׁר מֵשָׁר.** עֲרֻגּוֹת עֲרֻגּוֹת, וְכָל עֲרֻגָּה מִמִּין אֶחָד לְבַדּוֹ:


משר משר (to divide his field in straight lines/beds of various seeds – instead of square) -beds/grooves among beds/grooves and each bed/groove is from one species alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:2)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה תְלָמִים שֶׁל פָּתִיחַ.** כְּלוֹמַר מַנִּיחַ בֵּין מֵשָׁר לְמֵשָׁר רֶוַח שְׁלֹשָׁה תְלָמִים שֶׁל מַחֲרֵשָׁה, מִלְּשׁוֹן יְפַתַּח וִישַׁדֵּד אַדְמָתוֹ (ישעיה כח). וּבַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מְפָרֵשׁ שֶׁהֵם שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת רֹחַב עַל שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת אֹרֶךְ. וְאַחַר כָּךְ מֵצֵר וְהוֹלֵךְ אִם יִרְצֶה עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר בֵּינֵיהֶן בְּסוֹף הַמֵּצַר אֶלָּא כָּל שֶׁהוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּכָךְ הֵן נִרְאִים שֶׁלֹּא נִזְרְעוּ בְּעִרְבּוּבְיָא:


שלשה תלמים של פתיה (three ridges of a furrowed field) – that is to say, that he leaves between each bed/groove space of three ridges of a plough from the language (Isaiah 28:24): “breaking up and furrowing his land,” and in the Jerusalem Talmud it explains that they are two cubits wide by two cubits in length and afterwards he makes a boundary and goes on if he wants until there would not remain between them at the end of the boundary other than a little bit, for through this they appear that they were not sown in a mixture.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:3)


**הָעֹל הַשָּׁרוֹנִי.** כִּמְלֹא רֹחַב הָעֹל שֶׁחוֹרְשִׁים בּוֹ בַּשָּׁרוֹן, דְּהַיְנוּ בַבִּקְעָה, שֶׁהוּא רָחָב מִן הָעֹל שֶׁחוֹרְשִׁים בּוֹ בָּהָר:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:6:4)


**וּקְרוֹבִים דִּבְרֵי אֵלּוּ וְכוּ'.** שֶׁשִּׁעוּר שְׁלֹשָׁה תְלָמִים שֶׁל פָּתִיחַ קָרוֹב לִהְיוֹת שָׁוֶה לִמְלֹא הָעֹל הַשָּׁרוֹנִי:


וקרובים דברי אלו וכו' - that the measure of three ridges of a furrowed field is close to being equivalent to the width of a yoke used in the plain of Sharon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:1)


**תּוֹר.** הוּא שֵׁם תַּכְשִׁיט שֶׁל אִשָּׁה שֶׁצּוּרָתוֹ מְשֻׁלֶּשֶׁת, כְּלוֹמַר בַּעַל שָׁלֹשׁ זָוִיּוֹת, לְשׁוֹן תּוֹרֵי זָהָב נַעֲשֶׂה לָּךְ (שיר השירים א), וְעַל שְׁמוֹ קוֹרִין קֶרֶן זָוִית הַמְחֻדֶּדֶת רֹאשׁ תּוֹר. וְאִם קֶרֶן זָוִית שֶׁל שָׂדֶה שֶׁהִיא זְרוּעָה חִטִּים נִכְנֶסֶת בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֶה שֶׁל שְׂעוֹרִים מֻתָּר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא נִרְאֶה כְּסוֹף שָׂדֵהוּ וְנִכָּר שֶׁלֹּא נִזְרְעוּ בְּעִרְבּוּבְיָא:


תור – it is the name of an ornament of a woman whose form is triangular, meaning to say, that it has three angles, the language of (Song of Songs 1:11): “We will add wreaths of gold [to your spangles of silver],” and through it we call the horn of the juncture/corner-piece that is sharpened the head/point of the angle. But if the corner-piece/horn of the juncture of field sown with wheat enters into a field of barley it is permitted because it appears like the end of his field and it is recognized that it was not sewn in confusion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:2)


**מֻתָּר לִסְמֹךְ לוֹ מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין.** מֻתָּר לִסְמֹךְ אֵצֶל שְׂדֵה חִטִּין שֶׁלּוֹ מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין שֶׁל שְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ, וְאֵין נִרְאֶה כְּכִלְאַיִם בְּשָׂדֵהוּ מִשּׁוּם דְּנִרְאֶה כְּסוֹף שְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ:


מותר לסמוך לו מאותו המין – it is permitted to flank near his field of wheat from the same species of he field of his fellow, and it does not appear like mixed seeds/Kilayim in his field because it appears at the end of the field of his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:3)


**מֻתָּר לִסְמֹךְ לוֹ תֶּלֶם שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן.** שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁלֹּא זְרָעוּהוּ שָׁם אֶלָּא כְּדֵי לִבְדֹּק שָׂדֵהוּ אִם טוֹבָה לִזְרֹעַ בָּהּ פִּשְׁתָּן אִם לָאו, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אָדָם עָשׂוּי לִזְרֹעַ תֶּלֶם אֶחָד שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ בּוֹ שׁוּם תּוֹעֶלֶת. אֲבָל שֶׁל מִין אַחֵר דְּאָדָם עָשׂוּי לִזְרֹעַ מִמֶּנּוּ תֶּלֶם אֶחָד בִּלְבָד, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא זְרָעוֹ אֶלָּא לִבְדֹּק שָׂדֵהוּ אָסוּר, שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אוֹמֵר לְצָרְכּוֹ זְרָעוֹ כְּדֵי לֵהָנוֹת בּוֹ:


מותר לסמוך לו תלם של פשתן – for the person who sees knows that he did not sow it there other than to check his field if it is good to sow in it flax or not, because a person is not forced/pressured to sow one furrow of flax that he doesn’t have in it any profit/use. But of a different species that a person is forced to sow only one furrow, even if he didn’t sow it other than to check/examine [the suitability of the soil to grow flax in his field it is prohibited, for the person who sees states that he sowed it for his needs in order to derive benefit from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:4)


**אֶחָד זֶרַע פִּשְׁתָּן וְאֶחָד כָּל הַמִּינִין.** אָסוּר לִסְמֹךְ תֶּלֶם אֶחָד, שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ דְּלִבְדֹּק שָׂדֵהוּ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה:


אחד זרע פשתן ואחד כל המינין – it is forbidden to flank one furrow, for the person who sees does not know that he does it to check/examine his field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:7:5)


**מֻתָּר לִבְדֹּק בְּתֶלֶם שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן.** שֶׁהַכֹּל יוֹדְעִים שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּן לְקַיֵּם הַפִּשְׁתָּן אֶלָּא לִבְדֹּק שָׂדֵהוּ אִם הִיא יָפָה לְפִשְׁתָּן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


מותר לבדוק בתלם של פשתן - for everyone knows that he doesn’t intend to maintain the flax other than to check/examine [the suitability of the soil of] his field if it is good for flax. And the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah].



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:1)


**חָרִיעַ.** כַּרְכּוֹם יְעָרִי, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי אלקורטו"ם. וּשְׁנֵי מִינִים הַלָּלוּ דְּהַיְנוּ חַרְדָּל וְחָרִיעַ הֵן מַזִּיקִין לַתְּבוּאָה, וְאִם לֹא שֶׁבַּעַל הַשָּׂדֶה זְרָעָם וְרוֹצֶה בְקִיּוּמָם לֹא הָיָה מַנִּיחַ לְאָדָם אַחֵר שֶׁיִּזְרַע אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי אֵלּוּ הַמִּינִים סָמוּךְ לַתְּבוּאָה שֶׁלּוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ נִרְאֶה כִּמְקַיֵּם כִּלְאַיִם בַּשָּׂדֶה:


חריע – forest saffron, and we call it ELKORTUM in Arabic. These two species that is mustard and forest saffron/safflower damage grain and if the owner of the field did not sow them and desires their existence, he would not let another person to sow one of these two species near his grain, therefore it appears that he is maintaining mixed seeds in the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:2)


**אֲבָל סוֹמְכִין לִשְׂדֵה יְרָקוֹת.** שֶׁאֵינָן מַזִּיקִין לַיְרָקוֹת וְהָרוֹאֶה אוֹמֵר שֶׁל אָדָם אַחֵר הֵם וּבַעַל הַיְרָקוֹת אֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד עֲלֵיהֶן אִם הֵם סְמוּכִים לַיְרָקוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן מַזִּיקִין לָהֶן:


אבל סומכין לשדה ירקות – that do not do damage to vegetables and the person who sees it says that they belong to another person, and the owner of the vegetables is not strict/particular about them because they don’t damage them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:3)


**וְסוֹמֵךְ לְבוּר.** אִם יֵשׁ בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֶה שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה מְקוֹם בּוּר שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָרוּשׁ וְזָרוּעַ בֵּית רֹבַע אוֹ יוֹתֵר, סוֹמֵךְ שָׁם זֶרַע אַחֵר, וְכֵן סוֹמֵךְ אֵצֶל נִיר וְהוּא מָקוֹם חָרוּשׁ:


וסומך לבור (flanks an uncultivated land) – if there within the field of grain a place of uncultivated land which is not plowed nor sown a piece of ground of the capacity of one ROVA/quarter of seed, he flanks there another seed, and franks next to newly broken land and this is a plowed place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:4)


**וּלְגָפָה.** גֶּדֶר אֲבָנִים סְדוּרוֹת כְּעֵין חוֹמָה בְּלֹא טִיט:


ולגפה -a stone fence set up similar to a wall without plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:5)


**וּלְדֶרֶךְ.** אִם דֶּרֶךְ הַיָּחִיד מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּין שְׁתֵּי שָׂדוֹת, מֻתָּר לִזְרֹעַ מִין אֶחָד בְּזוֹ וּמִין אֶחָד בְּזוֹ:


ולדרך – if the individual path forms a partition between two fields, it is permissible to sow one species in this one and another species in that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:6)


**מֵסֵךְ עַל הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאֵין נוֹפוֹ גָּבוֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, אִם יֵשׁ תַּחְתָּיו זֶרַע מֻתָּר לִסְמֹךְ מִבַּחוּץ זֶרַע אֶחָד, דִּכְגָדֵר דָּמֵי:


מיסך על הארץ (which shades the ground) – that its bough is not higher than three handbreadths from the ground, if there is a seed underneath it, it is permitted to flank another seed from outside that is is like a fence.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:8:7)


**וּלְסֶלַע.** לֹא אִצְטָרִיךְ לְמִתְנְיֵהּ, דְּאֵין לְךָ מְחִצָּה גְּדוֹלָה מִזּוֹ, אֶלָּא לְאַשְׁמוּעִינַן דַּאֲפִלּוּ סֶלַע בָּעִינַן אַרְבָּעָה רֹחַב, דִּבְפָחוֹת מִכָּאן אֵין שְׁמָהּ מְחִצָּה לְהַפְסִיק:


ולסלע – it was not necessary to teach it [in the Mishnah], for there is no divider bigger than this, but rather to inform us that even a rock requires being four handbreadths wide for less than this, it is not called a divider to separate.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:1)


**קָרַחַת.** מָקוֹם פָּנוּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ זָרוּעַ כְּשֶׁהוּא מְרֻבָּע קָרוּי קָרַחַת, כְּמוֹ בְּקָרַחְתּוֹ (ויקרא יג) דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁנִּמְרַט הַשֵּׂעָר וְהַמָּקוֹם חָלָק:


קרחת – a free place that is not sown when it is quadrangular like (Leviticus 13:42): "בקרחתו"/”on the bald parr in the front,” which is the hair is plucked (i.e., made bald) and the place is smooth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:2)


**עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה קָרָחוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה.** וּבְכָל קָרְחָה זוֹרֵעַ מִין אֶחָד. וּבֵית סְאָה הוּא חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה עַל חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה, וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה בוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע קָרָחוֹת נִמְצֵאת כָּל קָרְחָה עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת וּשְׁנֵי טְפָחִים וּמֶחֱצָה אֹרֶךְ עַל עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת רֹחַב. כֵּיצַד, אִם תַּעֲשֶׂה מֵחֲמִשִּׁים עַל חֲמִשִּׁים עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ קָרָחוֹת תִּהְיֶה כָּל קָרְחָה עֶשֶׂר עַל עֶשֶׂר, קַח אַחַת מֵהֶן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּשָּׁאֲרוּ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע קָרָחוֹת וּתְחַלְּקֶנָּה לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע רְצוּעוֹת, נִמְצָא כָּל רְצוּעָה שְׁנֵי טְפָחִים וּמֶחֱצָה רֹחַב עַל אֹרֶךְ עֶשֶׂר, שֶׁכָּל אַמָּה הִיא בַת שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים טְפָחִים לְעֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, תֵּן כָּל רְצוּעָה מֵאֵלּוּ לְרֹאשׁ כָּל קָרְחָה, נִמְצֵאת כָּל קָרְחָה עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת וּשְׁנֵי טְפָחִים וּמֶחֱצָה אֹרֶךְ עַל עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת רֹחַב:


עשרים וארבע קרחות לבית סאה – and in every quadrangular patch [that is not sown] he sows one kind/ species. And a Bet Seah/a field requiring one Seah of seed is fifty cubits by fifty cubits, and when you make in it twenty-four patches, it is found that each quadrangular patch is ten cubits and two and one-half handbreadths length by ten cubits width. How so? If you make [in a field] from fifty [cubits] by fifty [cubits] , twenty five quadrangular patches, each quadrangular patch will be ten cubits by ten cubits; take one of them in order that twenty-four quadrangular patches will remain and divide them into twenty-four strips, it is found that each strip will be two and one-half handbreadths wide by a length of ten [handbreadths] for each cubit is six handbreadths which are sixty handbreadths, for ten cubits, give each strip from these at the head of each quadrangular patch, it is found that each quadrangular patch is ten cubits and two-and-one-half handbreadths length by ten cubits width.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:3)


**מִקָּרַחַת לְבֵית רֹבַע.** שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ בִּסְאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה רְבִיעִית שֶׁל קַב, תֵּן אוֹתָם לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה קָרָחוֹת, הֲרֵי לְכָל קָרְחָה בֵּית רֹבַע:


מקרחת לבית רובע – For in a Seah there are twenty-four square quarters of a Kab; place them for the twenty-four quadrangular patches so that each quadrangular patch will have one piece of ground of the capacity for one Rova of seed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:4)


**כָּל מִין שֶׁיִּרְצֶה.** וְלֹא מַצְרִיךְ רַבִּי מֵאִיר הַרְחָקָה כְּלָל דְּכֵיוָן דִּמְרֻבָּעוֹת הֵן נִרְאוֹת מֻפְרָשׁוֹת וּמֻבְדָּלוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


כל מין שירצה – but Rabbi Meir did not require distancing a all for since they are quadrangular, they appear as separated and distinguished one from another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:5)


**הָיְתָה.** בְּתוֹךְ שְׂדֵה תְבוּאָה קָרַחַת אַחַת שֶׁל בֵּית רֹבַע אוֹ שְׁתֵּי קָרָחוֹת:


היתה – within the field one quadrangular unsown patch of the capacity for one Rova of seed or wo quadrangular unsown patches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:6)


**זוֹרְעָם חַרְדָּל.** דְּאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כִּשְׂדֵה חַרְדָּל בְּתוֹךְ שְׂדֵה תְבוּאָה, אֲבָל שְׁלֹשָׁה קָרָחוֹת נִרְאוֹת כִּשְׂדֵה חַרְדָּל בְּתוֹךְ שְׂדֵה תְבוּאָה, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין רְגִילוּת לִזְרֹעַ מִן הַחַרְדָּל שָׂדֶה גְדוֹלָה. וּשְׁאָר מִינִים אֲפִלּוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה קָרָחוֹת אֵינָן נִרְאִין כְּשָׂדֶה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרְגִילִין לִזְרֹעַ מֵהֶן הַרְבֵּה בְּיַחַד:


זורעם חרדל – that it doesn’t appear like a field of mustard within a field of field of grain, but three quadrangular unsown patches appear like a field of mustard within the field of grain, because it is not normal to sow from the mustard a large field. But for the rest of the species, even three quadrangular unsown patches do not appear like a field because they regularly sow from them a lot together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים תֵּשַׁע קָרָחוֹת מֻתָּרוֹת.** אַרֵישָׁא קָמְהַדֵּר, דְּקָאָמַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר דְּזוֹרֵעַ כָּל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע קָרָחוֹת מִכָּל מִין שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים לִסְמֹךְ מִין אֵצֶל מִין אַחֵר, אֶלָּא עוֹשֶׂה לְבֵית סְאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ קָרָחוֹת דְּהַיְנוּ חָמֵשׁ שׁוּרוֹת, וְכָל שׁוּרָה מֵחָמֵשׁ קָרָחוֹת, וְזוֹרֵעַ קָרְחָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל שׁוּרָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וּמַנִּיחַ שְׁנִיָּה שֶׁאֶצְלָהּ בּוּרָה, וְזוֹרֵעַ שְׁלִישִׁית וּמַנִּיחַ רְבִיעִית בּוּרָה, וְזוֹרֵעַ חֲמִשִּׁית וּמַנִּיחַ שׁוּרָה שְׁנִיָּה כֻּלָּהּ בּוּרָה וְזוֹרֵעַ מִשּׁוּרָה שְׁלִישִׁית קָרְחָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וּשְׁלִישִׁית וַחֲמִשִּׁית וּמַנִּיחַ שׁוּרָה רְבִיעִית כֻּלָּהּ בּוּרָה וְזוֹרֵעַ מִשּׁוּרָה חֲמִשִּׁית קָרְחָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וּשְׁלִישִׁית וַחֲמִשִּׁית, הֲרֵי תֵּשַׁע קָרָחוֹת זְרוּעוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה:


וחכמים אומרים תשע קרחות מותרות - The Sages are replying to the first clause, for when Rabbi Meir staes that one sows all twenty-four quadrangular unsown patches from any species that he desires, but the Sages prohibit placing one species next to a different species , but rather one makes a Bet Seah/a field requiring one Seah of seed of twenty-five quadrangular unsown patches which are five rows and each row from the five quadrangular unsown patches and he sows the first quadrangular unsown patch of the first row and leaves the second that is next to as fallow ground and he sows the third and leaves the fourth as fallow ground and sows the fifth and leaves the second row entirely as fallow ground and sows from the third row the quadrangular unsown patch and the third and the fifth and leaves the entire fourth row as fallow ground and he sows from the fifth row the first, third and fifth quadrangular unsown patches , there are nine quadrangular unsown patches that are sown for a Bet Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:8)


**עֶשֶׂר אֲסוּרוֹת.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיְּהֵא מַפְסִיק בֵּין כָּל קָרַחַת וְקָרַחַת כְּשִׁעוּר קָרַחַת, דְּהַיְנוּ בֵּית רֹבַע בְּקֵרוּב. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


עשר אסורות – that there needs to be a partition/separation between each and every quadrangular unsown patch like the measurement of a quadrangular unsown patch which is the capacity for one Rova of seed in nearness. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:9:9)


**בֵּית כּוֹר.** שְׁלֹשִׁים סְאִים. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב לֹא אִתְפָּרֵשׁ:


בית כור – the language of Seahs. But the reason of Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov is not explained.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:1)


**כָּל שֶׁהוּא בְּתוֹךְ בֵּית רֹבַע.** כְּגוֹן נְקָעִים מְלֵאִים מַיִם, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָם רְאוּיִם לִזְרִיעָה עוֹלִים לְמִדַּת בֵּית רֹבַע לֵחָשֵׁב הֶרְחֵק בֵּין מִין וָמִין:


כל שהוא בתוך רובע – as for example, ravines filled with water, for even though they are not appropriate for sowing, they count for the measurement of a Bet Rova/a piece of ground of the capacity of one Rova of seed (i.e., one-quarter of a Kab) to be considered the distance between each and every species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:2)


**אֲכִילַת הַגֶּפֶן.** מַה שֶּׁהַגֶּפֶן תּוֹפֶסֶת וְאוֹכֶלֶת סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ, דְּהַיְנוּ כְּדֵי עֲבוֹדָתָהּ, עוֹלֶה לְמִדַּת בֵּית רֹבַע לְהַשְׁלִים עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת וּשְׁנֵי טְפָחִים וּמֶחֱצָה אֹרֶךְ עַל עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת רֹחַב, דְּאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר בֵּית רֹבַע שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהַפְסִיק בֵּין מִין לְמִין. וְכֵן הַקֶּבֶר נַמִּי עוֹלֶה. וְכֵן הַסֶּלַע, וּבְסֶלַע שֶׁאֵינוֹ גָבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה מַיְרֵי, דְּאִי גָּבוֹהַ עֲשָׂרָה הֲוֵי מְחִצָּה וּמַפְסִיק בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


אכילת הגפן – what the vine seizes and consumes around it, which is in order that it functions, counts for the measurement of a Bet Rova to complete the ten cubits and two-and-one-half handbreadths length by ten cubits width, as we stated above (in Mishnah 9) which is the measurement of a Bet Rova that one must form a partition between each species. And similarly, a grave also counts and similarly a rock, but we are speaking of a rock that is not ten [handbreadths] for if it is ten [handbreadths], it is a partition and separates by itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:3)


**תְּבוּאָה בִתְבוּאָה בֵּית רֹבַע.** הָיְתָה שָׂדֵהוּ זְרוּעָה חִטִּין וְרוֹצֶה לִזְרֹעַ בָּהּ שְׂעוֹרִים, צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק בֵּית רֹבַע, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ חִטִּין מַקִּיפִים אֶת הַשְּׂעֹרִים מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת, דְּהָתָם לֹא מְהַנְיָא הַרְחָקָה עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא פָּתוּחַ מֵרוּחַ אַחַת:


תבואה בתבואה בית רובע -if his field was sown with wheat and he wishes to sow in it barley, he needs to distance it a Bet Rova (i.e., a piece of ground the capacity of one Rova/one-quarter Kab of seed), as long as there won’t be any wheat surrounding the barley from the four directions for there the distance does not benefit until would be open from one direction.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:4)


**יָרָק בִּתְבוּאָה בֵּית רֹבַע.** וְדַוְקָא בִּמְרֻבָּע הוּא דְּבָעִינַן הָכִי, אֲבָל אִם רָצָה לַעֲשׂוֹת שׁוּרָה שֶׁל יָרָק בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֶה שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה, עוֹשֶׂה אֹרֶךְ שׁוּרָה שֶׁל עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת וּמֶחֱצָה עַל רֹחַב שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁהֶחְמִירוּ בִּמְרֻבָּע טְפֵי מִבְּשׁוּרָה, וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי:


ירק בתבואה בית רובע – and especially when it is quadrangular that we require this, but if he wanted to make one row of vegetables within the field of grain, he makes the length of the row of ten cubits and one-half by a width of six handbreadths for they were more stringent with a quadrangular [plot] than with a row, and this is what we stated in the Jerusalem Talmud.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:10:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יָרָק בִּתְבוּאָה שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים.** דְּסָבַר לֹא מַחְמְרִינַן בִּמְרֻבָּע טְפֵי מִבְּשׁוּרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר אומר ירק בתבואה ששה טפחים – for he holds that we are not stringent with a quadrangular [plot] more than that of a row. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:1)


**תְּבוּאָה נוֹטָה עַל גַּבֵּי תְבוּאָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּזְרְעוּ כְּהִלְכָתָן אֶלָּא שֶׁהַשִּׁבֳּלִים מִתּוֹךְ גָּדְלָן נִכְפָּפִים וְשׁוֹכְבִים זֶה עַל זֶה, וְכֵן כֻּלָּן:


תבואה נוטה על תבואה – as for example that they were sown according to Halakha but that sheaves within their growth are bent and lie one on top of the other, and similarly all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:2)


**חוּץ מִדְּלַעַת יְוָנִית.** שֶׁעָלִין שֶׁלָּהּ אֲרֻכִּים וּמִתְפַּשְּׁטִין בְּיוֹתֵר וּכְשֶׁהֵן נוֹטִין עַל גַּבֵּי תְבוּאָה אוֹ יָרָק הֵן מִסְתַּבְּכִין וּמִתְקַשְּׁרִין יוֹתֵר מִכָּל שְׁאָר מִינִין וּמֵחֲזוּ כִּלְאַיִם:


מדלעת יונית – that its leaves are long and stretch more than they lean on the grain or on the vegetables that they become interwoven and tied up more than the rest of the species and look like mixed seeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:3)


**וּפוֹל הַמִּצְרִי.** פשול"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


ופול המצרי – PESHULI in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 2:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/2:11:4)


**וְרוֹאֶה אֲנִי אֶת דִּבְרֵיהֶן מִדְּבָרָי.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן מְסַבְּכִין וּמִסְתַּבְּכִין כִּדְלַעַת יְוָנִית. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה שֶׁהַכֹּל מֻתָּר חוּץ מִדְּלַעַת יְוָנִית:


ורואה אני את דבריהן מדברי – that they do not have entangling but they become entangled like the Greek gourd. And such is the Halakha that everything is permitted except the Greek gourd.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/3:1:1)


**עֲרוּגָה שֶׁהִיא שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים עַל שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים וְכוּ'‏ זוֹרְעִים וְכוּ'.** בְּפֶרֶק רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא (שבת פד:) דָּרַשׁ לַהּ מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב כִּי כָאָרֶץ תּוֹצִיא צִמְחָהּ וּכְגַנָּה זֵרוּעֶיהָ תַצְמִיחַ (ישעיה סא), תּוֹצִיא חַד, צִמְחָהּ חַד, הֲרֵי תְּרֵי, זֵרוּעֶיהָ תְּרֵי הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה, תַצְמִיחַ חַד הֲרֵי חֲמִשָּׁה. וְהָא דִתְנַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק שֵׁנִי יָרָק בְּיָרָק שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים, הַנֵּי מִילֵי בְּשָׂדֶה גְדוֹלָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ יָרָק הַרְבֵּה אֲבָל הָכָא דְּאֵין זוֹרֵעַ מִכָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד אֶלָּא מְעַט לֹא חַיְשִׁינַן. וּמַה שֶּׁיֵּשׁ לְהַקְדִּים לְפֵרוּשׁ הַמִּשְׁנָה הוּא כִּי אֲנַחְנוּ צְרִיכִין שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֵּין כָּל זְרִיעָה וּזְרִיעָה רִחוּק טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה [הַגָּהָ"ה זֶה שֶׁלֹּא כְּדַעַת רַשִׁ"י וְהָרַאֲבַ"ד זַ"ל שֶׁהֵם זַ"ל סוֹבְרִים שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק בֵּין כָּל זֶרַע וְזֶרַע שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים. אֲבָל בְּשַׁבָּת פֶּרֶק רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא הֶעֱתִיק מִפֵּרוּשׁ רַשִׁ"י ז"ל דְּהָתָם כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּתוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב] כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִינְקוּ זֶה מִזֶּה, אֶלָּא אִם תִּהְיֶה זָוִיּוֹת בְּצַד זָוִיּוֹת אוֹ בְצַד צֶלַע מְקוֹם זְרִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה אָז לֹא יִצְטָרֵךְ, כִּי הַגְבָּלַת הַזָּוִיּוֹת יַרְאֶה הַבְדָּלָתָם וְאָז אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לִינִיקָתָם אֲפִלּוּ הֵם זֶה בְּצַד זֶה, וְאִם כֵּן בָּעֲרוּגָה שֶׁהִיא שִׁשָּׁה אֶפְשָׁר לִזְרֹעַ בָּהּ חֲמִשָּׁה מִינֵי זְרָעִים וְלֹא תִהְיֶה כִּלְאַיִם, וְזוֹ צוּרָתָהּ [צִיּוּר א'‏ בְּסוֹף הַמַּסֶּכֶת]. וְיִהְיֶה מִדַּת מַה שֶּׁנִּזְרַע בְּזוֹ הָעֲרוּגָה עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים, וְאָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא פֶּרֶק רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דְּבַעֲרוּגָה בֶּחָרָבָה שָׁנִינוּ, אֲבָל עֲרוּגָה בֵּין הָעֲרוּגוֹת אָסוּר לִזְרֹעַ בָּהּ חֲמִשָּׁה מִינִין, שֶׁאִם יִזְרַע בְּכָל רוּחַ שֶׁבַּעֲרוּגָה זוֹ וּבְכָל רוּחַ שֶׁבָּעֲרוּגָה שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹתֶיהָ יֵרָאֶה הַכֹּל כִּמְעֹרָב. וְהָרַב רַבִּי שִׁמְשׁוֹן זַ"ל פֵּרֵשׁ עַל פִּי הַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי דְּכָךְ הוּא סֵדֶר הַזְּרִיעָה, מַנִּיחַ בַּקְּרָנוֹת חָרֵב טֶפַח וַחֲצִי חֹמֶשׁ עַל טֶפַח וַחֲצִי חֹמֶשׁ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְרַחֵק זֶרַע מִזֶּרַע, זֶרַע מִזְרַח מִזֶּרַע דָּרוֹם, שִׁעוּר אֲלַכְסוֹן שֶׁל טֶפַח וַחֲצִי חֹמֶשׁ, דְּהַיְנוּ טֶפַח וָחֵצִי, וְזוֹרֵעַ בְּכָל רוּחַ אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים חָסֵר חֹמֶשׁ בָּאֹרֶךְ. וְרֹחַב טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁמֵּצֵר וְהוֹלֵךְ בְּכָל צַד עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא כָּלֶה לְרֹחַב טֶפַח חָסֵר חֹמֶשׁ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא לְעוֹלָם בֵּין זֶרַע דָּרוֹם לְזֶרַע מִזְרָח טֶפַח וָחֵצִי, וְכֵן לְכָל רוּחַ עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא מַנִּיחַ בְּאֶמְצַע הָעֲרוּגָה שְׁלֹשָׁה עַל שְׁלֹשָׁה, וְעוֹשֶׂה בָּאֶמְצַע נֶקֶב וְזוֹרֵעַ בּוֹ גַּרְעִין אֶחָד כָּזֶה [צִיּוּר ב'‏ בְּסוֹף הַמַּסֶּכֶת]. [הַגָּהָ"ה יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלֶה כָּל הַזָּרוּעַ שֶׁבְּצוּרָה זוֹ רַק פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁנֵים עָשָׂר טְפָחִים (נראה לי שצריך לומר פחות מארבעה עשר טפחים, דהוי שלשה עשר וארבעה חמשין)]. וְהִקְשׁוּ בַגְּמָרָא מַאי דוֹחְקֵיהּ לְאוֹקְמֵיהּ בָּעֲרוּגָה שֶׁבֶּחָרָבָה, יַקִּיפוּהָ עֲרוּגוֹת מִכָּל צַד כְּנֶגֶד הַקְּרָנוֹת וְאֵין בָּהּ דָּבָר זָרוּעַ, וְתֵרְצוּ גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יְמַלֵּא הַקְּרָנוֹת וְיִזְרַע הָעֲרוּגָה עַל זוֹ הַצּוּרָה [צִיּוּר ג'‏ בְּסוֹף הַמַּסֶּכֶת], אוֹ עַל זוֹ הַצּוּרָה [צִיּוּר ד'‏ בְּסוֹף הַמַּסֶּכֶת]. וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁלֹּא אָמְרוּ בַּעֲרוּגָה שֶׁהִיא שִׁשָּׁה עַל שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים זוֹרְעִין בְּתוֹכָהּ תִּשְׁעָה זֵרְעוֹנִין וְיִהְיֶה בֵּין כָּל אֶחָד טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה כָּזֶה [צִיּוּר ה'‏ בְּסוֹף הַמַּסֶּכֶת], לְפִי שֶׁכָּל הַנִּזְרָע מִמֶּנָּה הוּא תִּשְׁעָה טְפָחִים בִּלְבָד, וְעַל זוֹ שֶׁהִקְדַּמְנוּ יִהְיֶה עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים. וְהָא דְנָקַט עֲרוּגָה שִׁשָּׁה עַל שִׁשָּׁה, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא הַיּוֹתֵר קְטַנָּה שֶׁבָּעֲרוּגוֹת, שֶׁהִיא אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה, וְהִשְׁמִיעָנוּ שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לִזְרֹעַ בָּהּ זְרָעִים מִשְׁתַּנִּים וְלֹא יִהְיוּ כִלְאַיִם:


ערוגה שהיא ששה טפחים על ששה טפחים – it is explained below at the conclusion of the document. [if so, in all of the published editions, this commentary is not found at the conclusion of the document and now, when we have merited to the publication of the work Melekhet Shlomo (the work of Rabbi Shlomo Adani – 1567-1629 the 17th century scholar of Hebron), we found in it a copy of this commentary of Rabbi Obadiah of Bartenura with his thoughts. And in order that the teaching of the LORD is perfect [renewing life] (see Psalms 19:8), we have published it here in its appropriate place]. ערוגה – which is six handbreadths [by six handbreadths], etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/3:1:2)


**הָיָה לָהּ גּוֹבֵל גָּבוֹהַּ וְכוּ'.** בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי [הַגָּהָ"ה וְכֵן נִרְאֶה מִן הַבַּבְלִי פֶּרֶק רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא] אָמְרוּ שֶׁגַּם רֹחַב הַגּוֹבֵל טֶפַח, נִמְצֵאת הָעֲרוּגָה שְׁמֹנָה עַל שְׁמֹנָה:


היה לו גובל גבוה וכו' – in the Jerusalem Talmud [comment: and so it appears from the Babylonian Talmud in the ninth chapter of Shabbat/”Rabbi Akiva] they said that also the width of a boundary is a handbreadth, it is fund that the garden-bed is eight [handbreadths] by eight [handbreadths] and they sow in it thirteen [seeds]. Three on each and every boundary and one in the middle like this (see drawing #6 in the standard editions of the Mishnah), and it will be that the measurement of what is sown from it thirty-handbreadths. And the Rosh, of blessed memory (Rabbi Asher ben Yehiel 1250-1327 – born in Cologne, Germany and died in Toledo, Spain) explained that on each and every border he sows four [seeds] in the four corners and between each and every corner (comment: the rest of each boundary that is from one corner to another corner), [he sows] two kinds of seeds, and that which is taught in a Baraitha that three [seeds are sown] on each boundary because one [corner] is counted with the two and he counts one corner for every direction and between the four species that are in each direction, he has to needs to distance three [handbreadths of] blank space that between them are four and one-half handbreadths, and there will remain a place for seed that are four species of three and one-half handbreadths and this for each species a handbreadth minis one-eighth of a handbreadth. But in the Jerusalem Talmud an objection is raised that they place three [seeds] on every border and five in the middle like the garden-beds of the first clause and that there would be seventeen species. And it responds because the borders are excluded, meaning that one must distance the seed of the garden-bed from the seed of the border a handbreadth and-a-half [comment: it means a handbreadth-and-a-half from the eastern side and a handbreadth and-a-half from the western side, which are three handbreadths, and there doesn’t remain, etc.) but there does not remain a free space to sow in the garden-bed other than three handbreadths by three handbreadths. But if you would say, why doesn’t he sow on each and every border five species of six handbreadths for the four handbreadths of open space that is between the five species [of seed] and there would remain two handbreadths for the five species.
