[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:6:6)


**מִשֶּׁיִּשְׁקָע.** שֵׁם הַבְּעָלִים מִן הַכֶּרֶם וְיִקָּרֵא עַל שֵׁם הַגַּזְלָן, מֵאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה תִּהְיֶה הַכֶּרֶם חֲשׁוּבָה כְּשֶׁלּוֹ וִיקַדֵּשׁ כָּל מַה שֶּׁיִּזְרַע בָּהּ:


משישקע-[when it will settle] the name of the owners from the vineyard and it will be called by the name of the thief, from that hour the vineyard will be considered as his and he will sanctify all that he sows in it.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:1)


**הָרוּחַ שֶׁעִלְעֲלָה.** שֶׁסִּעֲרָה. רוּחַ סְעָרָה מְתַרְגְּמִינַן רוּחַ עַלְעוֹלָא:


הרוח שעלעלה – [a wind] that stormed, a windstorm we translate in Aramaic as a wind casting [grape vines over standing grain].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:2)


**יִגְדֹּר.** כְּמוֹ גּוֹדֵר בִּתְמָרִים, כְּלוֹמַר יַחְתֹּךְ וְיִלְקֹט:


יגדור -like cutting dates,, that is, he should sever and harvest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:3)


**וְכֵן בַּיָּרָק.** [הַנּוֹטֶה] תַּחַת הַגֶּפֶן:


וכן ירק – [that which hangs over] underneath the vine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:4)


**יַחֲזִיר.** הַתְּבוּאָה וְהַיָּרָק לִמְקוֹמָן:


יחזיר – [he will restore] the grain and vegetation to their places.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:5)


**וְאֵינוֹ מְקַדֵּשׁ.** אִם לֹא הֶחֱזִיר אֵינוֹ מְקַדֵּשׁ, דְּהָא דִתְנַן לְעֵיל תַּחַת הַגֶּפֶן אוֹסֵר וּמְקַדֵּשׁ הַיְנוּ כְּשֶׁעִקַּר הַתְּבוּאָה תַּחַת הַגֶּפֶן, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁעִקַּר הַתְּבוּאָה עוֹמֶדֶת חוּץ לַגֶּפֶן וְנוֹטָה תַּחַת הַגֶּפֶן אֵינוֹ מְקַדֵּשׁ:


ואינו מקדש – if he did not return, he does not sanctify it, for it is taught in the Mishnah above (see Tractate Kilayim, Chapter 7, Mishnah 3), underneath the vine it prohibits and he sanctifies it, that is when the root of the grain is underneath the vine, but when the vine of the grain stands over it outside of the vine and hangs over underneath the vine, he does not sanctify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:6)


**מִשֶּׁתַּשְׁרִישׁ.** מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁיִּתְפַּשְּׁטוּ שָׁרָשִׁים בַּקַּרְקַע. וְאִית דְּגָרְסֵי מִשֶּׁתַּשְׁלִישׁ כְּלוֹמַר מִשֶּׁתָּבִיא שְׁלִישׁ, דְּרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר (דברים כב) וּתְבוּאַת הַכֶּרֶם, וּפָחוֹת מִשְּׁלִישׁ לֹא מִקְרֵי תְּבוּאָה:


משתשריש – from the time that the roots spread out in the ground. But there are those who have the reading, "משתשליש" – that it should bring forth a third, for the All-Merciful stated (Deuteronomy 22:9): “and the yield of the vineyard,” but less than one-third is not called grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:7)


**וַעֲנָבִים מִשֶּׁיֵּעָשׂוּ כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן.** שֶׁאִם זָרַע תְּבוּאָה אוֹ יָרָק בְּכֶרֶם שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעוּ עֲנָבָיו לִהְיוֹת כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן וְעָקַר הַזֶּרַע קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ הָעֲנָבִים לִהְיוֹת כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן, הֲרֵי זֶה לֹא קִדֵּשׁ. וְכֵן עֲנָבִים שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן וְזָרַע בְּצִדָּן תְּבוּאָה אוֹ יָרָק וְלִקֵּט הַזֶּרַע קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּשְׁרִישׁ, אוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבִיא שְׁלִישׁ לְמַאן דְּגָרֵיס מִשֶּׁתַּשְׁלִישׁ, הֲרֵי זֶה לֹא קִדֵּשׁ:


וענבים משיעשו כפול הלבן – if he sowed grain or vegetation in a vineyard that its grapes with the stalk of grapes did not reach becoming like he hyacinth bean, and he uprooted the seed prior to it reaching the stalk of grapes to be like the hyacinth bean, this is not sanctified. And similarly, the stalk of grapes that became like the hyacinth bean and he sowed on their side grain or vegetation and harvested the seed prior to taking root, or prior to its bringing forth a third according to the one who had the reading of “that it should bring forth a third/משתשליש,” this is not sanctified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:7:8)


**תְּבוּאָה שֶׁיָּבְשָׁה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ וַעֲנָבִים שֶׁבָּשְׁלוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן.** וְנָטַע גֶּפֶן בְּצַד הַתְּבוּאָה שֶׁיָּבְשָׁה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ אוֹ זָרַע בְּצַד [הָעֲנָבִים שֶׁבָּשְׁלוּ] כָּל צָרְכָּן, אֵינָן מְקַדְּשׁוֹת, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (דברים כב) פֶּן תִּקְדַּשׁ הַמְּלֵאָה הַזֶּרַע אֲשֶׁר תִּזְרַע וּתְבוּאַת הַכֶּרֶם, אֵינוֹ מְקַדֵּשׁ עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שָׁם מְלֵאַת זֶרַע וּתְבוּאַת כֶּרֶם, וּתְבוּאָה וְיָרָק שֶׁלֹּא הִשְׁרִישׁוּ אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיאוּ שְׁלִישׁ לֹא הִגִּיעוּ לִכְלַל מְלֵאַת זֶרַע, וְכֵן לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּבְשׁוּ שׁוּב אֵינָן קְרוּיִן מְלֵאַת זֶרַע, וַעֲנָבִים קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ לִהְיוֹת כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן אוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּשְׁלוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן אֵינָן קְרוּיִין תְּבוּאַת כֶּרֶם:


וענבים שבשלו כל צרכן – and he planted a vine at the side of the grain that had withered all of its need, or sowed at the side [the grapes that had ripened] fully, they are not sanctified, for Scripture states (Deuteronomy 22:9): “else the crop – from the seed you have sown – and the yield of the vineyard may not be used, is not sanctified until there will be there the seed you have sown and the yield of the vineyard, but the grain and/or the vegetation that had not taken root or had not brought forth one-third did not reach the general principle of seed that you have sown – and similarly, after they withered, further, they are not called seed that you have sown, but grapes prior to their becoming like the hyacinth bean or after they have fully ripened, are not called the yield of the vineyard.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:1)


**עָצִיץ נָקוּב.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ נֶקֶב כְּדֵי שֹׁרֶשׁ קָטָן, וְזָרַע בְּתוֹכוֹ:


עציץ נקוב – that has in it a perforation in order that a small root and seed are inside of it (i.e., the perforated pot) [which prohibits the use of the vines when located in the vineyard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:2)


**מְקַדֵּשׁ בַּכֶּרֶם.** אִם הִנִּיחוֹ בַּכֶּרֶם אוֹ בְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת שֶׁל עֲבוֹדַת הַכֶּרֶם מְקַדֵּשׁ כְּאִלּוּ נִזְרַע בָּאָרֶץ:


מקדש בכרם – if he left it in a vineyard or four cubits of the tilling of the vineyard, he sanctifies it as if it was planted in the earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:3)


**אֲסוּרִין וְלֹא מְקַדְּשִׁין.** לֹא יָבִיא זֶרַע לְתוֹכוֹ לְכַתְּחִלָּה, וְאִם הֵבִיא לֹא קִדֵּשׁ, דְּלֹא הֲוֵי כְּזוֹרֵעַ בָּאָרֶץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אסורין ולא מקדשין – he should not bring seed into it ab initio, but if he brought and does not sanctify for it is not as if he is sowing in the earth. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/7:8:4)


**הַמַּעֲבִיר עָצִיץ נָקוּב בַּכֶּרֶם.** עָצִיץ נָקוּב שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ תְּבוּאָה שֶׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ, וְהֶעֱבִירוֹ בְּכֶרֶם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ עֲנָבִים כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן, וְלֹא הִנִּיחוֹ בָאָרֶץ אֶלָּא הֶעֱבִירוֹ תַּחַת הַגְּפָנִים בִּלְבָד, אִם הוֹסִיפוּ הַזְּרָעִים בְּדֶרֶךְ הַעֲבָרָתוֹ חֵלֶק מִמָּאתַיִם עַל מַה שֶּׁהָיוּ תְּחִלָּה, אָסוּר הַזֶּרַע שֶׁבֶּעָצִיץ, אֲבָל הַגְּפָנִים לֹא קָדְשׁוּ כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא הִנִּיחוֹ בַּקַּרְקַע. וּלְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק כֶּרֶם שֶׁחָרַב פֵּרַשְׁנוּ כֵּיצַד רוֹאִין אִם הוֹסִיף אֶחָד מִמָּאתַיִם:


המעביר עציץ נקוב בכרם – a perforated pot that has in it grain that has brought forth one-third growth, and he transferred it to a vineyard that has stalks of grapes like the hyacinth/white bean, he did not leave it in the ground but rather transferred it under the vines alone, if they added the seeds on the way of transferring part of two hundred on what they were from at the outset, the seed is prohibited in the common earthen vessel, but the vines that were not sanctified, since they were not left in the ground. But above in the chapter “The vineyard which lay waste/כרם שחרב“ (Chapter 5 of Tractate Kilayim we explained how we view things if he added one from two-hundred – see Mishnah 6).



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:1)


**כִּלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם אֲסוּרִין מִלִּזְרֹעַ.** אֲבָל אֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם עַד שֶׁיִּזְרַע שְׁנֵי מִינֵי זֶרַע שֶׁהֵן כִּלְאַיִם זֶה בָזֶה וְיִזְרְעֵם בַּכֶּרֶם, דְּהָכִי מַשְׁמַע קְרָא (דברים כב) לֹא תִזְרַע כַּרְמְךָ כִּלְאָיִם, לֹא תִזְרַע שְׁנֵי מִינֵי זְרָעִים שֶׁהֵן כִּלְאַיִם בְּתוֹךְ כַּרְמְךָ, וְאִם זְרָעוֹ בַּכֶּרֶם לוֹקֶה שְׁתַּיִם מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאֵי זְרָעִים וְכִלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם:


כלאי הכרם אסורים מלזרוע – but he is not flogged because of diverse kinds in a vineyard until he sows two kinds of seeds which are mixed seeds/Kilayim one to the other, and he sows them in the vineyard, for this is implied by the Scriptural verse (Deuteronomy 22:9): “You shall not sow your vineyard with a second kind of seed,” – you shall not sow two kinds of seeds which are mixed seeds/Kilayim within your vineyard, but if you sowed [them] in a vineyard, you are flogged twice because of [both] mixed seeds and because of diverse kinds of the vineyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:2)


**וּמִלְּקַיֵּם.** אִם רוֹאֶה אוֹתָם וּמִתְיָאֵשׁ מִלְּעָקְרָם אָסוּר, אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עוֹשֶׂה מַעֲשֶׂה:


ומלקיים – if he sees them and he gives up hope to uproot them [from the ground], it is prohibited , but he is not flogged other than if he commits a [positive] action.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:3)


**וַאֲסוּרִים בַּהֲנָאָה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) פֶּן תִּקְדַּשׁ, תּוּקַד אֵשׁ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהֵן טְעוּנִים שְׂרֵפָה וַאֲסוּרִים בַּהֲנָאָה:


ואסורים בהנאה – as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:9): “else the crop – from the seed you have sown – [and the yield of the vineyard may not be used],” [the Rabbis explained] that it is set on fire, teaching that they require burning and that they are prohibited from deriving benefit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:4)


**אֶלָּא לִלְבֹּשׁ.** וְהוּא הַדִּין לְהַעֲלוֹת עָלָיו, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יט) לֹא יַעֲלֶה עָלֶיךָ:


אלא ללבוש – and the same law applies about placing it upon oneself, as it is written (Leviticus 19:19): “you shall not put on cloth [from two kinds of mixture].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:5)


**אֶלָּא לְהַרְבִּיעַ.** לְהַכְנִיס כְּמִכְחוֹל בִּשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת, אֲבָל מֻתָּר לְהַכְנִיס לַדִּיר וּלְהַעֲמִיד זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה שְׁנֵי מִינִים זָכָר מִמִּין זֶה וּנְקֵבָה מִמִּין אַחֵר:


אלא להרביע – to forcibly with his strength bring them into a tube, but it is permit to bring them into a pen and to raise them two species one next to the other, a male of this species and a female of another species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:1:6)


**כִּלְאֵי בְהֵמָה אֲסוּרִים זֶה עִם זֶה.** כְּגוֹן הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַסּוּס שֶׁאֲבִיהֶן חֲמוֹר עִם הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַחֲמוֹר שֶׁאֲבִיהֶן סוּס. וּמַתְנִיתִין אַתְיָא כְּמַאן דְּאָמַר אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לְזֶרַע הָאָב, דִּלְמַאן דְּאָמַר חוֹשְׁשִׁין כָּל מִינֵי פְּרָדוֹת אַחַת הֵן וּמֻתָּרִים זֶה בָזֶה. וּבַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי רָצוּ לְהוֹכִיחַ שֶׁאָסוּר לִרְכֹּב עַל פִּרְדָה מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר, וּמָה אִם בִּבְגָדִים שֶׁאַתָּה מֻתָּר לִלְבֹּשׁ זֶה עַל גַּבֵּי זֶה אַתָּה אָסוּר בְּתַעֲרֻבְתָּן, בְּהֵמָה שֶׁאַתָּה אָסוּר לְהַנְהִיג זוֹ עִם זוֹ לֹא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁאַתָּה אָסוּר בְּתַעֲרֻבְתָּהּ, וּמַקְשִׁינַן וְהָכְתִיב (שמואל ב יג) וַיִּרְכְּבוּ אִישׁ עַל פִּרְדּוֹ וַיָּנֻסוּ, אֵצֶל בְּנֵי דָוִד כְּשֶׁנֶּהֱרַג אַמְנוֹן, וּמְשַׁנִינַן אֵין לְמֵדִין מִן הַמַּלְכוּת. תּוּ מַקְשִׁינַן וְהָא כְּתִיב (מלכים א א) וְהִרְכַּבְתֶּם אֶת שְׁלֹמֹה בְנִי עַל הַפִּרְדָּה אֲשֶׁר לִי, וּמְשַׁנִינַן הַהִיא בְּרִיָּה מִשֵּׁשֶׁת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית הַוְיָא. וּמִיהוּ חָזֵינָא לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן קְשִׁישֵׁי קַמָּאֵי דְקַמָּן שֶׁרוֹכְבִין לִפְנֵיהֶם עַל פְּרָדוֹת וְאֵין מוֹחֶה בַּדָּבָר, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּזְהִירִים שֶׁלֹּא לְהַנְהִיג יַחַד שְׁתֵּי פְּרָדוֹת דְּשֶׁמָּא אַחַת מֵהֶן נוֹלְדָה מֵחֲמוֹר וְאָבִיהָ סוּס, וְהָאַחֶרֶת מִסּוּס וְאָבִיהָ חֲמוֹר:


כלאי בהמה אסורים זה עם זה – as for example those born from a horse whose father was a donkey with those born of a donkey whose father was horse, but our Mishnah brings according to the opinion that we are not concerned about the seed of the father, for one who states that we are concerned about all kinds of mules are one, they are permitted one to the other. But in the Jerusalem Talmud, they (i.e., the Rabbis) wanted to prove that it is prohibited to ride on a mule from an a fortiori, for just as if regarding clothing you are permitted to wear this on top of that (i.e., mixed seeds), [but] you are prohibited in their mixture, an animal in which you are prohibited from directing/leading this one with that one, is it not all the more so, that you are prohibited in its mixture, and we raise the following difficulty: Isn’t it written (2 Samuel 13:29): “whereupon the other princes mounted their mules and fled,” regarding the sons of David when Amnon was killed, but we teach that one cannot learn from the [family] of the kingdom, further, we raise the difficulty, but surely it is written (1 Kings 1:33): “[The king said to them: ‘Take my loyal soldiers] and have my son Solomon ride on my mule [and bring him down to Gihon],’” and we respond that this creature is from the six days of creation. But however, we see that for the Rabbis that those who are older before me – that they ride before him first on mules and they did not protest in the matter, but rather, that they warn [them’ that they should not lead together two mules, for perhaps one of them was born from a donkey and its father was a horse, and the other was from a horse and is father was a donkey.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:2:1)


**בְּהֵמָה עִם בְּהֵמָה וְחַיָּה עִם חַיָּה וְכוּ'.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא תַחֲרֹשׁ בְּשׁוֹר וּבַחֲמֹר (דברים כב) אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא, יָלְפִינַן שׁוֹר שׁוֹר מִשַּׁבָּת דְּהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף:


בהמה עם בהמה וחיה עם חיה וכו' – even though that the All-Merciful stated (Deuteronomy 22:10): “You shall not plow with an ox and an ass together,” we derive [from an analogy] "שור"\"שור" (Deuteronomy 22:10 and Deuteronomy 5:14: “[you shall not do any work – you, your son or your daughter, your male or female slave,] your ox or your ass, or any of your cattle,” from Shabbat (see also Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 5, Mishnah 7) and the same law applies for all the cattle, wildlife and birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:2:2)


**וְלִמְשֹׁךְ וּלְהַנְהִיג.** לִמְשֹׁךְ אֶת שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לִמְשֹׁךְ וּלְהַנְהִיג אֶת שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לְהַנְהִיג, גָּמָל אָרְחֵיהּ בִּמְשִׁיכָה וַחֲמוֹר אָרְחֵיהּ בְּהַנְהָגָה. וּמִיהוּ בֵּין בְּגָמָל בֵּין בַּחֲמוֹר חַיָּב בֵּין מוֹשֵׁךְ בֵּין מַנְהִיג:


ולמשוך ולהנהיג – to pull that which it is customary to pull/drawing and to lead where it is customary to lead; a camel its manner is to pull and a donkey its manner is through leading. But however, whether [speaking] of a camel or [speaking] of a donkey, one is liable, whether by pulling/drawing or whether by leading.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:1)


**וְהַיּוֹשֵׁב בַּקָּרוֹן.** חַיָּב הוֹאִיל וְאָזְלֵי מֵחֲמָתֵיהּ חָשֵׁיב כְּאִלּוּ מַנְהִיגָן:


והיושב בקרון – is liable since they go as a consequence/from the effect of his actions, it is considered as if he leads them (i.e., the animals).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:2)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר פּוֹטֵר.** הַיּוֹשֵׁב בַּקָּרוֹן דְּלָאו מִידֵי קָעֲבִיד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


רבי מאיר פוטר – the person who sits in a wagon does not do anything. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:3:3)


**וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית שֶׁהִיא קְשׁוּרָה לָרְצוּעוֹת.** אִם שְׁנֵי סוּסִים קְשׁוּרִים בַּקָּרוֹן וְקָשַׁר שָׁם חֲמוֹר, אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְלֹא חֲמוֹר קָאָזְלֵי אָסוּר, וְלֹא חָזִינַן לַחֲמוֹר כְּמַאן דְּלֵיתֵיהּ:


והשלישי שהוא קשורה לרצועות – if two horses are tied to the wagon and he tied there (i.e., to the harness) a donkey, even though that without the donkey they are going, it is prohibited, but we don’t see the donkey as something that isn’t there.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:1)


**אֵין קוֹשְׁרִין סוּס בְּצִדֵּי הַקָּרוֹן.** כְּשֶׁהַבָּקָר מוֹשְׁכוֹת בַּקָּרוֹן אֵין קוֹשְׁרִין סוּס בְּצִדּוֹ אוֹ אַחֲרָיו שֶׁל קָרוֹן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּלְמַד לִמְשֹׁךְ, לְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים הַסּוּס שֶׁאַחַר הַקָּרוֹן דּוֹחֵף אֶת הַקָּרוֹן וּמַנְהִיגוֹ בְּגוּפוֹ:


אין קושרין סוס בצדי הקרון – when the oxen/cattle pull the wagon we don’t tie a horse to hits side or behind the wagon in order that it will learn to pull, because sometimes, the horse that is behind the wagon pushes the wagon and leads it with its body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:2)


**לֻבְדְּקִים.** חֲמוֹר שֶׁל לוּד, וְהוּא גָּדוֹל וְעָרִיץ מִשְּׁאָר חֲמוֹרִים וְדוֹמֶה לְגָמָל:


לובדקם (Lybian ass) – a donkey of Lydda and it is large and powerful than the rest of the donkeys/asses and is similar to a camel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:3)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲבִיהֶן חֲמוֹר מֻתָּרִין זֶה עִם זֶה.** שֶׁאָבִיו שֶׁל זֶה חֲמוֹר וְאָבִיו שֶׁל זֶה חֲמוֹר. וְקָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא אָמְרִינַן אָתֵי צַד סוּס וּמִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּצַד חֲמוֹר וְצַד חֲמוֹר מִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּצַד סוּס, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאִמּוֹתֵיהֶן שָׁווֹת אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לְזֶרַע הָאָב:


אע"פ שאביהן חמור מותרין זה עם זה – the father/sire of this is a donkey and the father of that one is donkey. And this comes to inform us that we don’t say that it comes at the same of a horse and has intercourse at the side of a donkey/ass and the side of the donkey has intercourse with the side of an a horse, for since their mothers are the same, we don’t fear for the seed of the father/sire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:4:4)


**אֲבָל הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַחֲמוֹר עִם הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַסּוּס אֲסוּרִים זֶה עִם זֶה.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁאִמּוֹ שֶׁל זֶה מִמִּין הַחֲמוֹרִים וְאִמּוֹ שֶׁל זֶה מִמִּין הַסּוּסִים, דְּבָתַר זֶרַע הָאֵם אָזְלִינַן וַהֲרֵי הֵן שְׁנֵי מִינִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אבל הנולדים מן החמור עם הנולדים מן הסוס אסורים זה עם זה – since the mother of this one is from the species of the donkeys and the mother of that one is from the species of horses, and follow go after the seed of the mother, and they are two different species. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:1)


**פְּרוּטִיּוֹת.** מִין פְּרָדוֹת שֶׁאֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לַעֲמֹד [עֲלֵיהֶן] וּלְבָרֵר עַל יְדֵי סִימָנִים אֵיזֶה מֵהֶם אִמּוֹ חֲמוֹר וְאֵיזֶה מֵהֶם אִמּוֹ סוּס, הִלְכָּךְ אֲסוּרִין זֶה עִם זֶה:


פרוטיות (a sort of hybrids – mules of which it is unknown whether their sires were horses and their mothers asses or the reverse) – a species of mules that a person is unable to stand [upon them] and to verify through signs which of them its mother is a donkey and which of them its mother is horse, therefore, they are prohibited one with the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:2)


**וְהָרַמָּךְ מֻתָּר.** שֶׁכֻּלָּם מִין אֶחָד וְאִמָּם סוּס, וּבִלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי קוֹרִין לְסוּסְיָא נְקֵבָה רמ"ך, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ (אסתר ח) בְּנֵי הָרַמָּכִים:


והרמך מותר (but the mule of a horse dam/rammakh gender with its kind – i.e., a mule foaled by a horse) – for they are all of one sex and their mothers are a horse, and in the Arabic language they call a female horse RAMAKH, and similar to it are the offspring of the mule of a horse dam.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:3)


**אַדְנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה.** חַיָּה הַגְּדֵלָה בַּשָּׂדוֹת, וּכְמִין חֶבֶל גָּדוֹל יוֹצֵא מִן הָאָרֶץ שֶׁגְּדֵלָה בוֹ אוֹתָהּ חַיָּה, וּשְׁמָהּ יָדוֹעַ, וְהוּא יִדְעוֹנִי הַכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה, וּמְחֻבָּר בְּטַבּוּרוֹ בְּאוֹתוֹ חֶבֶל הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הָאָרֶץ, וְצוּרָתוֹ צוּרַת אָדָם בְּפַרְצוּף וְיָדַיִם וְרַגְלַיִם וְאֵין כָּל בְּרִיָּה רַשָּׁאָה לִקְרַב אֵלָיו כִּי הוּא הוֹרֵג וְטוֹרֵף כָּל הַקָּרֵב אֵלָיו. וּכְשֶׁרוֹצִים לָצוּד אוֹתוֹ מוֹרִים בְּחִצִּים בַּחֶבֶל עַד שֶׁנִּפְסָק, וְצוֹעֵק בְּקוֹל מַר וּמֵת מִיָּד, וְאֵלָיו רָמַז בְּאִיּוֹב (ה) כִּי עִם אַבְנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה בְּרִיתֶךָ:


אדני השדה (name of a mythical animal, orangoutang)– a large wildlife in the fields , and like a kind of large rope that comes out from the ground in which this wildlife was raised, and its name is known, and it is the charmer/soothsayer that is written in the Torah, and it is attached at its navel with that rope that goes out of the ground, and its form is the form of a man in its face and hands and feet, but no creature is permitted to approach it for it kills and tears all who are near it. Bug when they wish to hunt it, they shoot arrows with a rope until it is severed and it cries out in a bitter voice and dies immediately. And there is a hint concerning it in Job (5:23): “For you will have a pact with the rocks in the field, [and the beasts of the field will be your allies].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:4)


**מְטַמֵּא בָּאֹהֶל כָּאָדָם.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע עַל פְּנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה, בָּאָדָם שֶׁגָּדֵל בַּשָּׂדֶה:


מטמא באהל כאדם – as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “And in the open, anyone who touches a person,” like a person who grew up in the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:5)


**הַקֻּפָּד.** שֶׁרֶץ שֶׁגּוּפוֹ מָלֵא קוֹצִים, וּכְשֶׁאָדָם נוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ כּוֹפֵל עַצְמוֹ וּמַכְנִיס יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו בְּבִטְנוֹ וְנַעֲשֶׂה כְּכַדּוּר, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בְּלַעַ"ז אריצ"ו:


הקופד (hedgehog) – a reptile whose body is full of thorns and when a person touches it, it folds itself and brings in its hands and feet in its stomach and becomes like a ball, and we call it in the foreign tongue, ARITZO.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:6)


**חֻלְדַּת הַסְּנָיִים.** חֻלְדָּה הַגְּדֵלָה בַּסְּנֶה:


חולדת הסניים (porcupine) – a weasel that grows in a bush.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:5:7)


**כַּזַּיִת בְּמַשָּׂא.** דִּמְסַפְּקָא לְהוּ אִם מִין חַיָּה וּמְטַמְּאָה נִבְלָתוֹ בְּכַזַּיִת וּמְטַמְּאָה נַמִּי טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה בְּמַשָּׂא לְטַמֵּא אָדָם לְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים כְּנִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה, אוֹ מִין שֶׁרֶץ הוּא וּמְטַמֵּא בְּכָעֲדָשָׁה אֲבָל אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּמַשָּׂא כְּנִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה אֶלָּא בְּמַגָּע כְּשֶׁרֶץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי לֹא בְאַדְנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה וְלֹא בְחֻלְדַּת הַסְּנָאִים:


כזית במשא (an olive’s bulk when caried) – that it was doubtful to them if it is kind of wildlife and that its carcass defiles by an olive’s bulk, and that it is defiles also in a graver manner through carrying to defile humans, to defile clothing like the carcass of an impure animal, or it is kind of unclean reptile and it defiles in like a lentil but it doesn’t defile by carrying like the carrion of an animal, but rather through contract like an unclean reptile. Bug the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi neither with regard to the large wildlife of the fields nor like the porcupine.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:1)


**שׁוֹר הַבָּר.** שׁוֹר הַגָּדֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר:


שור בר – an ox that grew up in the desert (i.e., wild ox).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:2)


**מִין בְּהֵמָה.** וְחֶלְבּוֹ אָסוּר, וְאֵינוֹ טָעוּן כִּסּוּי, דְּסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן עִקָּרוֹ מִן הַיִּשּׁוּב הָיָה אֶלָּא שֶׁבָּרַח לַמִּדְבָּר וְנַעֲשָׂה שׁוֹר בָּר:


מן בהמה – but its milk is forbidden but it doesn’t require an act of covering, for the Rabbis hold that it was uprooted from civilization but that it fled to the wildness and became a wild ox/a desert ox.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:3)


**מִין חַיָּה.** וְטָעוּן כִּסּוּי, וְחֶלְבּוֹ מֻתָּר, קָסָבַר עִקָּרוֹ מִן הַמִּדְבָּר הָיָה. וְסִימָנִים שֶׁמָּנוּ חֲכָמִים לְהַכִּיר בֵּין בְּהֵמָה לְחַיָּה בִּשְׁאָר חַיּוֹת נֶאֶמְרוּ חוּץ מִשּׁוֹר הַבָּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


מן חיה – and it requires covering but its milk is permitted, for he holds that it was uprooted from the wilderness. But the signs that the Sages counted to recognize between a domesticated animal and wild animals in the rest of the creatures, it was stated, except for the wild ox. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:4)


**כֶּלֶב מִין חַיָּה.** נַפְקָא מִנַּהּ לְכוֹתֵב כָּל חַיָּה שֶׁבִּרְשׁוּתוֹ לְאַחֵר, אוֹ לְמַקְדִּישׁ כָּל חַיָּה שֶׁבִּרְשׁוּתוֹ:


כלב מן חיה – the practical difference to one who writes all wildlife that is in his domain to someone else, or that he dedicates all of the wildlife that is in his domain/possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:5)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר מִין בְּהֵמָה.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ר' מאיר אומר מן בהמה – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:6)


**עָרוֹד.** חֲמוֹר הַבָּר:


ערוד – wild ass/the donkey of the wilderness/desert.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 8:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/8:6:7)


**וְאָדָם מֻתָּר עִם כֻּלָּם.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) לֹא תַחֲרֹשׁ בְּשׁוֹר וּבַחֲמֹר, אֲבָל אַתָּה חוֹרֵשׁ בְּאָדָם וְשׁוֹר, בְּאָדָם וַחֲמוֹר:


ואדם מותר עם כולם – as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:10): “You shall not plow with an ox and an ass together,” but you can plow with a man and an ox, with a man and a donkey/ass.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:1)


**אֵין אָסוּר מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם אֶלָּא צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) לֹא תִלְבַּשׁ שַׁעַטְנֵז צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים. שַׁעַטְנֵז כְּלָל, צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים פְּרָט, כְּלָל וּפְרָט אֵין בַּכְּלָל אֶלָּא מַה שֶּׁבַּפְּרָט, צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים אִין, מִידֵי אַחֲרִינָא לֹא. וְצֶמֶר הוּא צֶמֶר רְחֵלִים וְאֵילִים בִּלְבָד, דִּכְתִיב (מלכים ב ג) וּמֵישַׁע מֶלֶךְ מוֹאָב הָיָה נֹקֵד וְהֵשִׁיב לְמֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאָה אֶלֶף כָּרִים וּמֵאָה אֶלֶף אֵילִים צָמֶר, אֵין לְךָ קָרוּי צֶמֶר סְתָם אֶלָּא צֶמֶר אֵילִים, אֲבָל שְׁאָר צֶמֶר צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן שֵׁם לְוַי, צֶמֶר גְּמַלִּים צֶמֶר אַרְנָבִים צֶמֶר עִזִּים:


אין אסור משום כלאים אלא צמר ופשתים – as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:11): “You shall not wear cloth combining wool and linen.” [The word] שעטנז is a generalization; צמר ופשתים /wool and linen is a specification/detail; a generalization and a specification/detail, the generalization refers only to that which was specified in the details. Wool and Linen, yes, [are forbidden together]; something else is not [forbidden together]. But צמר/wool is the wool of sheep or rams alone, as it is written (2 Kings 3:4): “Now King Mesha of Moab was a sheep breeder; and he used to pay as tribute to the king of Israel the wool of one hundred thousand lambs and of one hundred thousand rams.” You do not have it called mere wool, but rather the wool of rams, but he rest of wool, one needs to give an epithet, the wool of camels, the wool of hares, the wool of goats.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בִנְגָעִים אֶלָּא צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְהַבֶּגֶד כִּי יִהְיֶה בוֹ נֶגַע צָרָעַת בְּבֶגֶד צֶמֶר אוֹ בְּבֶגֶד פִּשְׁתִּים:


ואינו מטמא בנגעים אלא צמר ופשתים – as it is written (Leviticus 13:47): “When an eruptive affection occurs in a cloth of wool or linen fabric.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:3)


**אֵין הַכֹּהֲנִים לוֹבְשִׁים לְשַׁמֵּשׁ בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ אֶלָּא צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים.** דִּבְבִגְדֵי כֹהֲנִים כְּתִיב שֵׁשׁ וּבָד, וְהוּא פִּשְׁתָּן שֶׁעוֹלֶה בַּד בְּבַד, כְּלוֹמַר קָנֶה יְחִידִי מִכָּל גַּרְעִין וְאֵין שְׁנֵי קָנִים עוֹלִים מִגַּרְעִין אֶחָד. וּבָאַבְנֵט כְּתִיב תְּכֵלֶת וְאַרְגָּמָן וְתוֹלַעַת שָׁנִי. וּתְכֵלֶת, צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ בְּדַם חִלָּזוֹן. וְאַרְגָּמָן, צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ בְּצֶבַע אָדֹם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ אַרְגָּמָן וְהוּא לאכ"א בְּלַעַ"ז. וְתוֹלַעַת שָׁנִי, הוּא צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ בְּצֶבַע אָדֹם שֶׁקּוֹרִין כרמז"י:


אין הכהנים לובשים לשמש במקדש אלא צמר ופשתים – for regarding the clothing of the priests, it is written (Exodus 39:27-29): “They made the tunics of fine linen of woven work, [for Aaron and his sons];…and the linen breaches of fine twisted linen,” which is a flax that grows in linen stalks, that is to say, a singular red from each seed, but no two reeds grow from one seed. And regarding the sashes/אבנט (verse 29), it is written (Exodus 39:29): “blue and purple and crimson yarns, [done in embroidery -as the LORD had commanded Moses].” “And blue/תכלת” -the wool that is colored with the blood of the purple-shell used for dying tekhelet, “and purple/ארגמן”- that is colored by the color ed whose name is ארגמן/purple, which is LAKA in the foreign tongue; and “crimson yarns/תולעת שני” which is colored in the color red that they call CARMAZI.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:4)


**שֶׁטְּרָפָן.** שֶׁעֵרְבָן:


שטרפן – that mixed them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:5)


**מֻתָּר.** לְעָרֵב עִמָּהֶן פִּשְׁתָּן לְפִי שֶׁצֶּמֶר רְחֵלִים נִתְבַּטֵּל בְּרֹב שֶׁל גְּמַלִּים:


מותר – to mix flax with them because the wool of lambs/sheep is nullified through the majority of [wool of] camels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:1:6)


**וְכֵן פִּשְׁתָּן וְקַנְבּוֹס.** שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ וְהָרֹב מִן הַקַּנְבּוֹס מֻתָּר לְהָבִיא צֶמֶר וּלְעָרֵב עִמָּהֶם:


וכן פשתן וקנבוס – that became combined – and the majority is from the hemp, it is permitted to bring wool and to mix with them.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:1)


**הַשִּׁירָיִים וְהַכָּלָךְ.** מִינִים שֶׁל מֶשִׁי הֵן, וְהַכָּלָךְ גָּדֵל בִּכְרַכֵּי הַיָּם וְתַבְנִיתוֹ כְּתַבְנִית הַזָּהָב, וְהוּא רַךְ בְּיוֹתֵר וְדוֹמֶה לְצֶמֶר:


השיריים והכלך (silk and cissaros-blossoms/a woolly substance growing on stones on the Dead Sea that looks like gold, and being very soft – resembles sheep-wool) – they are species of silk, and the כלך/cissaros-blossoms/ a wooly substance) grows in the sea-towns/mercantile ports and its shape/form is like the shape of gold and it is exceptionally soft and similar to wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:2)


**אֲבָל אֲסוּרִים.** דְּאָתֵי לְאַחְלוֹפֵי בְּצֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מְצוּיִין בֵּינֵיהֶם לֹא הָיוּ מַכִּירִים בָּהֶן וְאָתֵי לְאַחְלוֹפֵי, הִלְכָּךְ אֲסוּרִים מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעַיִן. אֲבָל הָאִידְנָא שֶׁהַמֶּשִׁי מָצוּי בֵּינֵינוּ וְהַכֹּל יוֹדְעִים וּמַכִּירִים בּוֹ אֵין אָסוּר שׁוּם מִין שֶׁל מֶשִׁי בְּצֶמֶר אוֹ בְּפִשְׁתִּים, וְכֵן הַקַּנְבּוֹס שָׁרֵי בֵּין בְּצֶמֶר בֵּין בְּפִשְׁתִּים, שֶׁהַכֹּל מַכִּירִים בּוֹ:


אבל אסורים – for they come o be exchanged/switched with wool and linen. But because they are were not found among them , people would not recognize them and they would come to switch/exchange them, therefore, they are prohibited for appearance’s sake. But nowadays, that the silk is found amongst us and all know and recognize it, there is no prohibition of any species of silk with wool or linen. And similarly, hemp is permitted whether with wool or with linen, for everyone recognize it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:3)


**הַכָּרִים וְהַכְּסָתוֹת אֵין בָּהֶם מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁהֵן עֲשׂוּיִין לִשְׁכִיבָה, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה (ויקרא יט) לֹא יַעֲלֶה עָלֶיךָ, אֲבָל אַתָּה מֻתָּר לְהַצִּיעוֹ תַּחְתֶּיךָ. וְהַנֵּי מִילֵי כְּשֶׁהֵן קָשִׁים וְרֵקָנִים וּנְתוּנִים עַל גַּבֵּי אִצְטַבָּא, אָז הֵן מֻתָּרִין כְּשֶׁאֵין בְּשָׂרוֹ [נוֹגֵעַ] בָּהֶם, אֲבָל אִם הֵם רַכִּים אוֹ מְלֵאִים אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ רֵקָנִים וּמֻנָּחִים עַל גַּבֵּי מִטָּה, אֲפִלּוּ עֶשֶׂר מַצָּעוֹת זוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי זוֹ וְכִלְאַיִם תַּחְתֵּיהֶם אָסוּר לִישֹׁן עָלֶיהָ, לְפִי שֶׁנִּכְפָּפִים תַּחְתָּיו וְנִכְרָכִים עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ:


הכרים והכסתות אין בהם משום כלאים – because they are made for ling on and the Torah stated (Leviticus 19:19): “you shall not put on cloth [from a mixture of two kinds of material],” but you are permitted to insert them underneath you. But these words [are applicable] when they are hard and thin and placed on top of a couch, then they are permitted when one’s skin doe not [touch] them, but if they are soof or filled or even empty and placed on top of a bed, even ten mattresses one on top of the other with mixed seeds underneath them, it is prohibited to sleep upon them because they are bent underneath him and they are wrapped on his skin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:4)


**אֵין עֲרַאי לְכִלְאַיִם.** דִּלְבִישַׁת עֲרַאי לְבִישָׁה הִיא:


אין עראי לכלאים – for temporary dressing [of mixed seeds] is dressing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ עַל גַּבֵּי עֲשָׂרָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶהֱנֶה מִן הַכִּלְאַיִם:


אפילו על גבי עשרה – and even though he does not benefit from the mixed seeds [from these ten garments].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:2:6)


**אֶת הַמֶּכֶס.** רָאִיתִי לִקְצָת מֵרַבּוֹתַי שֶׁפֵּרְשׁוּ כְּגוֹן בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהַיְּהוּדִים פּוֹרְעִים מֶכֶס לַמּוֹכֵס הָעוֹמֵד מֵאֵלָיו, וְלוֹבֵשׁ כִּלְאַיִם כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַכִּיר שֶׁהוּא יְהוּדִי. וְלִי נִרְאֶה לִגְנֹב אֶת הַמֶּכֶס לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אָדָם נוֹתֵן מֶכֶס מִבְּגָדִים שֶׁהוּא לוֹבֵשׁ. וּסְתָם מִשְׁנָה זוֹ [סָבְרָה] דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּן אָסוּר, וּפְלִיגָא אַאִידָךְ סְתָמָא דִתְנַן (לקמן משנה ה) מוֹכְרֵי כְסוּת מוֹכְרִין כְּדַרְכָּן:


את המכס – I saw that for some of my teachers that explained, for example, that in a place where the Jews pay customs to the custom-collector who stands from near him , and he wears mixed seats in order that he should not be recognized that he is a Jew (see Tractate Bava Kamma 113a). But it appears to me to steal the customs, because a person a person does not give customs from the clothing that he wears. But [the teacher of] this anonymous Mishnah [holds] that a thing that he doesn’t intend upon is prohibited, and he disputes upon the other anonymous teacher who teaches (see Mishnah 5 of this chapter) that clothes dealers sell [garments of diverse kinds] in their usual manner.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:1)


**מִטְפְּחוֹת הַיָּדַיִם.** שֶׁמְּנַגְּבִים בָּהֶם כְּשֶׁנּוֹטְלִים אֶת הַיָּדַיִם:


מטפחות הידים – [hand towels] that one dries their hands with when they ritually wash their hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:2)


**מִטְפְּחוֹת הַסְּפָרִים.** שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים עַל גַּבֵּי סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה:


מטפחות הספרים – that they place on the Torah scroll.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:3)


**וּמִטְפְּחוֹת הַסַּפָּג.** שֶׁמְּנַגֵּב בָּהֶם כָּל גּוּפוֹ אַחַר שֶׁרָחַץ:


ומטפחות ספג – that one dries with them all of his body after he bathed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:4)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר אוֹסֵר.** מִטְפְּחוֹת יָדַיִם לְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים מְחַמֵּם בָּהּ יָדָיו, וּמִטְפְּחוֹת סְפָרִים פְּעָמִים שֶׁמְּשִׂימָהּ בְּחֵיקוֹ וּמִתְחַמֵּם, וּמִטְפְּחוֹת הַסַּפָּג פְּעָמִים שֶׁרוֹאֶה אֶת רַבּוֹ וּמִתְעַטֵּף בָּהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר:


ר' אלעזר אוסר – [Rabbi Elazar prohibits] hand towels because sometimes he warms his hands with them, and scroll wrappers sometimes, he places it on his chest and becomes warm, and towels, sometimes that he sees his Rabbi and wraps himself up in it. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Elazar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:5)


**מִטְפְּחוֹת סַפָּרִים.** הַמְגַלְּחִים בְּנֵי אָדָם, וּפוֹרְסִים עֲלֵיהֶם מִטְפַּחַת כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְלַכְלְכוּ בִגְדֵיהֶם:


מטפחות ספרים (barbers’ towels) – that they shave people, and spread upon them a towel in order that their clothes do not become dirty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:3:6)


**אֲסוּרִים מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאָיִם.** וּבְהָא מוֹדֶה תַּנָּא קַמָּא, לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן בֵּית רֹאשׁ וְלוֹבְשִׁים אוֹתָן דֶּרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ, וְהָכִי מוּכָח בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי:


אסורים משום כלאים – and in this the first [anonymous] Tanna agrees because they have a have a top and we wear them like clothing. And this is proved in the Jerusalem Talmud.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:4:1)


**תַּכְרִיכֵי הַמֵּת.** שֶׁהַמֵּת נִקְבָּר בָּהֶם, אֵין בָּהֶן מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם, דִּכְתִיב (תהלים פח) בַּמֵּתִים חָפְשִׁי, כֵּיוָן שֶׁמֵּת אָדָם נַעֲשֶׂה חָפְשִׁי מִן הַמִּצְוֹת:


תכריכי המת – [the shrouds] that the dead is buried in them, they don’t have [the obligation to observe the Mitzvah of] Kilayim/mixed seeds as it is written (Psalms 88:6): “abandoned among the dead, [like bodies lying in the grave of whom You are mindful no more,” because a person died, he becomes free from [observing] all of the commandments (see Tractate Niddah 61b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:4:2)


**וּמַרְדַּעַת חֲמוֹר.** הִיא קָשָׁה יוֹתֵר מִכָּרִים וּכְסָתוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ אֲפִלּוּ בְּשָׂרוֹ נוֹגֵעַ לֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תִּכָּרֵךְ נִימָא עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהַכִּלְאַיִם נִכָּרִים וְנִרְאִים בָּהּ, אֲבָל בֶּגֶד שֶׁאָבַד בּוֹ כִּלְאַיִם לֹא יַעֲשֶׂנּוּ מַרְדַּעַת לַחֲמוֹר, דְּחַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִקַּח מִמֶּנּוּ טְלַאי לְבִגְדּוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְאֵין הַכִּלְאַיִם נִכָּרִים:


ומרדעת חמור – this is harder than mattresses and cushions, therefore, even if his skin touches, we are not troubled by it lest a shred of the garment used as a mattress winds itself around his body, and especially, when the mixed seats are recognized and appear in it, but a piece of clothing that where the mixed seeds are beyond recognition, he should not make it into a pack saddle for a donkey, for we are concerned lest he take from it a piece of cloth/patch for his clothing, since the mixed seeds are not recognized.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:1)


**מוֹכְרֵי כְסוּת מוֹכְרִים כְּדַרְכָּן.** לוֹבְשִׁים בִּגְדֵי כִלְאַיִם לְהַרְאוֹת לַקּוֹנֶה מִדַּת אָרְכּוֹ וְרָחְבּוֹ, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְכַּוְּנוּ לַהֲנָאָתָן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִם מִתְכַּוֵּן אִיכָּא אִסּוּרָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, כִּי לֹא מִתְכַּוֵּן שָׁרֵי לְכַתְּחִלָּה. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


מוכרי כסות מוכרים כדרכן – they wear clothing containing mixed seeds to demonstrate to the purchaser the measurement of its length and width, as long as they don’t intend to have them (i.e., the purchasers) benefitting from them, for even though if he intends [to do so], there is the prohibition from the Torah, but if he does not intend [for purchasers to violate the Torah regulation], it is permissible ab initio. And such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:2)


**וְהַצְּנוּעִים.** הַמַּחְמִירִים עַל עַצְמָן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:5:3)


**מַפְשִׁילִין בְּמַקֵּל.** נוֹתְנִים הַמַּקֵּל עַל כִּתְפֵיהֶם וְהַכִּלְאַיִם מֻנָּחִים בְּקָצֵהוּ וְאֵין כִּתְפֵיהֶם נוֹגְעוֹת בַּכִּלְאַיִם:


מפשילין במקל (the scrupulous carry the mixed material garment in bundles over their shoulders) – they place the staff on their shoulders and the [garment of] mixed seeds are placed at its end, and their shoulders do not touch the mixed seeds.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:6:1)


**תּוֹפְרִין כְּדַרְכָּן.** מַנִּיחִין הַכִּלְאַיִם עַל הַבִּרְכַּיִם וְעַל הַשּׁוֹקַיִם כְּשֶׁהֵן תּוֹפְרִים:


תופרין כדרכן – they place the mixed on the knees and the legs when they are sewing.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:1)


**הַבַּרְסִין וְהַבַּרְדְּסִין.** שְׁנֵיהֶם עֲשׂוּיִין מִצֶּמֶר וּמְכַסִּים בָּהּ אֶת הַמִּטּוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵלּוּ דַּקִּין וְאֵלּוּ עָבִין:


הברסין והברדסין (birrus – a cloak of thick woolen material and Brundisian cloaks) – both are made from wool and they cover with it the beds but some of them are thin and others are thick.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:2)


**דַּלְמְטִיקְיוֹן.** בָּתֵּי שׁוֹקַיִם, קאלצי"ש בְּלַעַ"ז:


דולמטיקיון (dalmatic) - wool pants covering the legs; KAKTZISH in the foreign tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:3)


**מִנְעַל הַפִּנּוֹן.** מִנְעָל שֶׁעוֹשׂוֹת מִפְּסֹלֶת הַצֶּמֶר:


מנעל הפינון (felt shoes) – shoes that are made from the refuse of wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:4)


**עַד שֶׁיִּבְדֹּק.** שֶׁמָּא יֵשׁ פִּשְׁתָּן מְעֹרָב בָּהֶן:


עד שיבדוק – lest perhaps there is flax mixed in them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:5)


**הַבָּאִים מֵחוֹף הַיָּם וּמִמְּדִינַת הַיָּם.** מֵחוֹף הַיָּם שֶׁהוּא מִמְּדִינַת הַיָּם:


הבאים מחוף הים וממדינות הים – from the seacoast which is from abroad.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:6)


**שֶׁחֶזְקָתוֹ מִן הַקַּנְבּוֹס.** מִשְׁנָה זוֹ נִשְׁנֵית בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה בִּזְמַן שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה הַפִּשְׁתָּן מָצוּי בְּכָל מָקוֹם, אֲבָל בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה שֶׁהַפִּשְׁתָּן מָצוּי בְּכָל מָקוֹם כֻּלָּם צְרִיכִים בְּדִיקָה, הִלְכָּךְ הַלּוֹקֵחַ בְּגָדִים תְּפוּרִים מִן הַנָּכְרִי בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה לֹא יִלְבַּשׁ עַד שֶׁיִּבְדֹּק יָפֶה:


שחזקתו מן הקנבוס – this Mishnah is teaching at first about a time when flax/linen was not found in every place, but at the present time where flax/linen is found everywhere, everyone needs to examine, therefore, a person who purchases clothing sewn by a heathen in the present time shojld not wear it until he examines it well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:7)


**וּמִנְעָל שֶׁל זֶרֶב.** מִנְעָלִים שֶׁל עוֹר וּמְחֻפִּים בִּפְנִים בִּלְבָדִים אוֹ בְצֶמֶר עָב וּמַגִּיעִין עַד הַיָּרֵךְ, כְּעֵין אוֹתָן שֶׁקּוֹרִין בורזקי"ש בְּלַעַ"ז וּבַעֲרָבִי כופ"ש. וּלְפִי שֶׁעֲשׂוּיִן לְחַמֵּם הָרֶגֶל קְרוּיִין שֶׁל זֶרֶב, לְשׁוֹן יְזֹרְבוּ נִצְמָתוּ (איוב ו):


ומנעל של זרב (cloth-lined shoe) – leather shoes which are covered inside with felt or with thick wool and reach until the leg, like those that they call in the foreign tongue BORZKISH or in Arabic KUPASH, and because they are made to warm the foot, they are called זרב /cloth-lined as is the language (Job 6:17): “But when they thaw, they vanish.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:7:8)


**אֵין בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאָיִם.** אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין שֶׁמָּא פִּשְׁתִּים מְעֹרָב בָּהֶם:


אין בהם משום כלאים – we are not concerned lest linen/flax is combined/mixed in with them.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:1)


**שׁוּעַ טָווּי וָנוּז.** שׁוּעַ סָרוּק בַּמַּסְרֵק וְשׁוּעַ חָלָק. אִישׁ חָלָק מְתַרְגְּמִינַן גְּבַר שְׁעִיעַ:


שוע טווי ונוז (hackled, spun or weaved) – שוע – is combed/hatched with a comb and שוע/hackled is smooth. A smooth [skinned] man (i.e., like Jacob, see Genesis 27:11 when he says to his mother Rebecca: “But my brother Esau is a hairy man and I am smooth-skinned”) which we translate in Aramaic as גבר שעיע/smooth-skinned man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:2)


**נוּז.** שָׁזוּר, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁנֵי חוּטִין שְׁזוּרִין יַחַד. וְעִקַּר כִּלְאַיִם מִן הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַצֶּמֶר סָרוּק וְטָווּי וְשָׁזוּר לְבַדּוֹ, וְכֵן הַפִּשְׁתָּן סָרוּק וְטָווּי וְשָׁזוּר לְבַדּוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִהְיוּ אֲרוּגִים יַחְדָּו אוֹ מְחֻבָּרִים יַחַד בִּשְׁתֵּי תְכִיפוֹת, דַּהֲווּ כְּאָרִיג. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי אִצְטְרִיךְ קְרָא לְמִשְׁרֵי כִּלְאַיִם בְּצִיצִית, לְפִי שֶׁחוּטֵי תְכֵלֶת שְׁזוּרִים הֵם דִּכְתִיב פְּתִיל תְּכֵלֶת, טָווּי וְשָׁזוּר, וְכֵן חוּט לָבָן, וּכְשֶׁמְּחַבְּרָן יַחַד בִּשְׁתֵּי תְכִיפוֹת הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם גְּמוּרִים דְּאוֹרַיְתָא. וְהָא דִתְנַן הָכָא אֵין אָסוּר מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם אֶלָּא טָווּי וְאָרוּג שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים כב) לֹא תִלְבַּשׁ שַׁעַטְנֵז, לָאו לְמִדְרָשׁ אָרִיג מִשַּׁעַטְנֵז, דְּאָרִיג מִיַּחְדָּו נַפְקָא, דְּדָרְשִׁינַן מִנֵּיהּ דְּאַף שְׁתֵּי תְכִיפוֹת הֲוֵי חִבּוּר, וְלֹא מַיְתֵי שַׁעַטְנֵז אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם טָווּי. וְרָאִיתִי לְאֶחָד מֵרַבּוֹתֵינוּ שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ שׁוּעַ טָווּי הַצֶּמֶר לְבַד וְהַפִּשְׁתִּים לְבַד, וָנוּז כְּלוֹמַר וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוּז דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם אֲרוּגִים יַחַד, וְהָכִי מִתְיַשֵּׁב לִישְׁנָא דְמַתְנִיתִין שַׁפִּיר טְפֵי, וְהָכִי מַשְׁמַע בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי. וּפֵרוּשִׁים הַרְבֵּה מָצָאתִי לְרַבּוֹתַי בְּפֵרוּשׁ שׁוּעַ טָווּי וָנוּז, וְכוּלְהוּ אִית לְהוּ פִּרְכָא לְבַד מֵהַאי דְּלֵית לֵיהּ פִּרְכָא:


נוז – twisted, that is two threads twisted together. And the essential principle from the Torah about Kilayim/mixed seeds is that the wool will be combed and spun and weaved alone. And similarly, the linen will be combed and spun and weaved alone, and after that, they will be weaved/plaited together or combined together with two stitches that are like a weaving. And because of this, Scripture needed to permit Kilayim/mixed seeds in Tzizit/ritual fringes because the threads of Tekhelet are woven, as it is written (Numbers 15:38): “let them attach a cord of blue [to the fringe at each corner],” spun and woven, and similarly the white string, when they combine them together with two stitches, this is complete Kilayim/mixed seeds according to the Torah. But it is taught in the Mishnah hear, that there is no prohibition because of mixed seeds/Kilayim other than spun or woven, as it states (Deuteronomy 22:11): “You shall not wear cloth combining wool and linen,” not for expounding a Midrash is weaving from Shaatnez/mixed cloth, for weaving is from “together”/יחדו (end of Deuteronomy 22:11) it is derived, for we expound from it that even two stitches are a combination, and we don’t bring Shaatnez other than because of spinning. But I saw that one of our Rabbis who explained that hackled or combed/שוע of he wool alone and the linen is alone, and weaving/נוז, that is to say, and afterwards that is both of them woven together and this fits well with the language of the Mishnah extremely well, and this is what is implied in the Jerusalem Talmud and in many commentaries that I found of my Rabbis/teachers as an explanation to שוע טווי ונוז/hackled, spun or weaved. But all of them have a refutation outside of that which doesn’t not have a refutation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:8:3)


**נָלוֹז וּמֵלִּיז.** עָקֹם וּמְעַקֵּם דַּרְכֵי אָבִיו שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם עָלָיו. לְשׁוֹן אַל יָלִיזוּ מֵעֵינֶיךָ (משלי ג):


נלוז ומליז – curved and someone who is tortuous in the ways of his father that is in heaven up on him. It is the language of (Proverbs 3:21): “My son, do not lose sight of them.”



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:1)


**הַלְּבָדִים.** צֶמֶר טָרוּף בְּלֹא טְוִיָּה וַאֲרִיגָה וְעוֹשִׂין מִמֶּנּוּ בֶּגֶד. וְאִם טָרַף צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים יַחַד וְעָשָׂה מֵהֶן בֶּגֶד אֲסוּרִים מִדְּרַבָּנָן, דְּדַוְקָא מִן הַתּוֹרָה הוּא מַצְרִיךְ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן שׁוּעַ טָווּי וָנוּז, אֲבָל מִדְּרַבָּנָן בְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ סַגִּי אוֹ שׁוּעַ אוֹ טָווּי אוֹ נוּז, וְהָנָךְ כֵּיוָן שֶׁהֵם שׁוּעִין אֲסוּרִים מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם:


הלבדים (felted stuff/cloaks) – hackled/combed wool without spinning and weaving, and they make from it clothing. But if he combined/mixed wool and linen together and made from them clothing, they are prohibited from the Rabbis, for specifically from the Torah it requires that all three of them are hackled, spun and weaved, but from the Rabbis one of these is enough, or hackled/combed, or spun or woven, and these since they are hackled, they are forbidden from their (i.e., the Rabbis’) words.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:2)


**פִּיו שֶׁל צֶמֶר בְּשֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן.** הָאוֹרְגִים אַחַר שֶׁמַּשְׁלִימִים הַבֶּגֶד רְגִילִים לַעֲשׂוֹת עַל שְׂפָתוֹ [שְׁלֹשָׁה] וְאַרְבָּעָה חוּטִין עָב כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְקַיֵּם הַבֶּגֶד, וְהוּא קָרוּי פִּיו, וְאִם הַבֶּגֶד שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן אָסוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת פִּיו שֶׁל צֶמֶר:


פיו של צמר בשל פשתן (fringe of wool fastened onto [garment] of linen) – the weaving after they complete the clothing that is customary to make on its rim and four thick threads in order that the clothing will endure, and it is called a fringe [knotted to the border], but if the clothing is of linen, it is forbidden to make its fringe [knotted to the border] of wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:3)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחוֹזְרִין לָאָרִיג.** מְסַבְּבִין אֶת הָאָרִיג. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר דּוֹמִין לָאָרִיג:


מפני שחוזרין לאריג (because the threads [of the fringe] interlace the web [of the garment]) – for they surround the web/weaving. Another interpretation: they are similar to the web/weaving.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:4)


**מְשִׁיחוֹת שֶׁל אַרְגָּמָן.** יְתָרִים וַחֲבָלִים שֶׁל צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ אַרְגָּמָן:


משיחות של ארגמן (ribbons of purple)– that which remains over and ropes of purple colored wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:5)


**אֲסוּרוֹת.** לַחְגֹּר עַל גַּבֵּי חָלוּק שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן:


אסורות – [it is forbidden] to gird to top of a cloak of linen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:6)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מוֹלֵל עַד שֶׁלֹּא קוֹשֵׁר.** שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְחַבֵּר בִּמְלִילָה הַחֲגוֹר שֶׁל צֶמֶר עִם הֶחָלוּק שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּקְשֹׁר בָּהּ מָתְנָיו:


מפני שהוא מולל עד שלא קושר (because he hems [the purple band] before he knots it) – for I is their custom to attach through hemming the wool outfit with the linen cloak prior to tying its loins.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:7)


**לֹא יִקְשֹׁר סֶרֶט שֶׁל צֶמֶר בְּשֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן.** שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה אָדָם חֲגוֹרוֹת שֶׁל צֶמֶר וַחֲגוֹרוֹת שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן וְיִקְשְׁרֵם זֶה בָזֶה:


ולא יקשור סרט של צמר בשל פשתן – a person should not make belts/girdings of wool and belts/girdings of linen and tie them to each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:9:8)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָרְצוּעָה בָאֶמְצַע.** כְּגוֹן רְצוּעָה שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁמְּשִׁיחוֹת שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן בְּרֹאשָׁהּ אֶחָד וּמְשִׁיחוֹת שֶׁל צֶמֶר בְּרֹאשָׁהּ הָאַחֵר, וּכְשֶׁהוּא חוֹגֵר בָּהּ קוֹשֵׁר שְׁנֵי רָאשֶׁיהָ שֶׁל צֶמֶר וְשֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן זֶה בָזֶה. דְּאִי לָאו הָכִי פְּשִׁיטָא דְשָׁרֵי, שֶׁהֲרֵי מַעֲשִׂים בְּכָל יוֹם דִּבְגָדִים שֶׁל צֶמֶר מְשִׂימִים תַּחְתֵּיהֶם עוֹרוֹת טְלָאִים שֶׁהֵם תְּפוּרִים בְּפִשְׁתָּן:


אע"פ שהרצועה באמצע – as for example, a belt of wool that here are bands of linen at one end and bands of wool at the other end and when he girds it, he ties the two heads/ends of wool and of linen with each other, for if this were not the case, it is obvious that it is permitted, for they are the daily actions regarding clothing of wool that hey place underneath them skins of cloth used as a blanket when they are sewn in linen.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:1)


**אוֹתוֹת הַגַּרְדִּיִּים.** דֶּרֶךְ הָאוֹרְגִים וְהַכּוֹבְסִים לִרְשֹׁם שֵׁם הַבְּעָלִים עַל הַבֶּגֶד בְּמַעֲשֵׂה רוֹקֵם כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְחַלְּפוּ שֶׁל אֶחָד בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ. וְאִם הַבֶּגֶד שֶׁל צֶמֶר וְאוֹתוֹת שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן אוֹ אִפְּכָא, אָסוּר מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם:


אותות הגרדיים – it is the manner of the weavers and launders to mark the name of the owners on the clothing in an embroidered work in order that one’s [clothing] will not get switched with that of his fellow. But if the clothing is of wool and the marks are of linen or the opposite, it is forbidden because of Kilayim/mixed seeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:2)


**תְּכִיפָה אַחַת.** תְּחִיבָה אַחַת שֶׁתָּחַב הַמַּחַט בַּבֶּגֶד פַּעַם אַחַת בִּלְבָד:


תכיפה א' (one stitch) – one stitch that that the needle inserted in the clothing one time alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:3)


**אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר.** לְטֻמְאָה וּלְטָהֳרָה, וְאִם נִטְמָא אֶחָד מֵהֶם לֹא נִטְמָא חֲבֵרוֹ, וְאִם הִזָּה עַל אֶחָד מֵהֶם לֹא הִזָּה עַל חֲבֵרוֹ:


אינו חבור – for ritual impurities and spiritual purity. BU t if one of them became defiled, its neighbor/partner was not defiled, and if he sprinkled upon one of them, he didn’t sprinkle on its neighbor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:4)


**וְאֵין בָּהּ מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם.** אִם אֶחָד שֶׁל צֶמֶר וְאֶחָד פִּשְׁתִּים וְחִבְּרָם בִּתְכִיפָה אַחַת:


ואין בה משום כלאים – if one is of wool and the other is linen and he combined them in one stitch.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:5)


**וְהַשּׁוֹמְטָהּ בְּשַׁבָּת.** עַל מְנָת לַחֲזֹר וְלִתְפֹּר פָּטוּר, וְלֹא הֲוֵי כְּקוֹרֵעַ עַל מְנָת לִתְפֹּר שְׁתֵּי תְפִירוֹת דְּחַיָּב:


והשומטה בשבת (he who undoes the fastening on Shabbat) – in order to return and to sow, he is exempt, for it is not like tearing in order to sow two stitches where one is liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:6)


**עָשָׂה שְׁנֵי רָאשֶׁיהָ לְצַד אֶחָד.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֶעֱבִיר הַמַּחַט פַּעַם אַחַת וְלֹא הֶעֱבִיר כָּל הַחוּט, וְהֶעֱבִיר הַמַּחַט פַּעַם שֵׁנִית וְנִמְצְאוּ שְׁנֵי רָאשֶׁיהָ לְצַד אֶחָד וּקְשָׁרָן, דְּאִם לֹא קָשַׁר שְׁנֵי רָאשֵׁי הַחוּט יַחַד אֵינוֹ מִתְקַיֵּם:


עשה שתי ראשים לצד א' (brought both ends of the thread to one side) – as for example, he transferred the needle once but did not transfer all of the thread but transferred the needle a second time, and it was found that he two ends [of the thread] are on on side and tied them, but if he didn’t tie the tw ends together, it is not enduring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:7)


**עַד שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּשׁ.** שֶׁיַּכְנִיס וְיוֹצִיא וְיַחֲזֹר וְיַכְנִיס שְׁלֹשָׁה פְעָמִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


עד שישלש – that he would bring in and take out and return and bring in three times [i.e., one complete and one incomplete stitch]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Kilayim 9:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Kilayim/r/9:10:8)


**הַשַּׂק וְהַקֻּפָּה מִצְטָרְפִין לְכִלְאָיִם.** אִם חֲתִיכַת בֶּגֶד צֶמֶר מְחֻבֶּרֶת לַשָּׂק וַחֲתִיכַת בֶּגֶד פִּשְׁתִּים מְחֻבֶּרֶת לַקֻּפָּה, וְחִבְּרָן יַחַד בִּשְׁתֵּי תְכִיפוֹת, מִצְטָרְפוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן מְחֻבָּרוֹת לִשְׁנֵי כֵלִים, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן הַאי לְחוּדֵיהּ קָאֵי וְהַאי לְחוּדֵיהּ קָאֵי וְאֵינָן חִבּוּר. וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם מְחֻבָּרִים לִשְׁאָר כֵּלִים דַּהֲוֵי דִּינָן כְּמוֹ שַׂק וְקֻפָּה:


השק והקופה מצרפין לכלאים – if one piece of wool clothing is attached to a sack and [and another] piece of linen clothing is attached to a basket, and he attached them together with two stitches, they combine, even though they are not attached to two utensils, and we don’t say that this one stands alone and that one stands alone and are not connected. And the same law applies if they are attached to the rest of the utensils, for their law is like that of a sack and a basket.
